LinearStudy.exists_common_polynomial_normal_coordinates.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {K : Type u_1} {σ : Type u_2} {ι : Type u_3}
  [Field K] [Infinite K] [Fintype σ] [DecidableEq σ] [Fintype ι] {c : ℕ} (ν : Fin c → σ) (hν : Function.Injective ν)
  (G : ι → Fin c → MvPolynomial σ K) (x : ι → σ → K)
  (hG : ∀ (a : ι), LinearIndependent K fun i j => (MvPolynomial.eval (x a)) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv j) (G a i))) :

LinearStudy.constructed_normal_target_tangent_parameters.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {K : Type u_1} {α : Type u_2} {β : Type u_3}
  [Field K] [Fintype α] [Fintype β] [DecidableEq α] [DecidableEq β] (I : Ideal (MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K)) (x : α ⊕ β → K)
  (hIP : I ≤ RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval x).toRingHom) (G : β → MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K)
  (hs :
    Ideal.map
        (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K) (Localization.AtPrime (RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval x).toRingHom))) I =
      Ideal.span
        (Set.range fun i =>
          (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K) (Localization.AtPrime (RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval x).toRingHom)))
            (G i)))
  (h0 : ∀ (i : β), (MvPolynomial.eval x) (G i) = 0)
  (hJ : IsUnit (Matrix.det fun i j => (MvPolynomial.eval x) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) (G i)))) :
  ∃ M,
    ∃ (hM : M.det ≠ 0),
      let Q := RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval (M⁻¹.mulVec x)).toRingHom;
      let I' := Ideal.map (LinearStudy.polynomialLinearChangeEquiv M hM).toRingEquiv.toRingHom I;
      ∃ (hIQ : I' ≤ Q),
        let J := Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K) (Localization.AtPrime Q)) I';
        Ideal.span
            (Set.range fun i =>
              (Ideal.Quotient.mk J)
                ((algebraMap (MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K) (Localization.AtPrime Q))
                  (MvPolynomial.X (Sum.inl i) - MvPolynomial.C (M⁻¹.mulVec x (Sum.inl i))))) =
          IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal (Localization.AtPrime Q ⧸ J)
'LinearStudy.constructed_normal_target_tangent_parameters' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

      (MvPowerSeries (Fin (n + 1)) (MvPowerSeries (Fin (r + 1)) F) ⧸ Ideal.span (Set.range H))] :

    (M.mulVec MvPowerSeries.X = fun i => LinearStudy.parameterSpecialization (H i)) ∧

            1 ∧

  (ψ : LinearStudy.CoordinateRing n →+* LinearStudy.AmbientRing r c) (hr : 0 < r) (hc : 0 < c)
  (H : Fin c → LinearStudy.AmbientRing r c)
  (hcoord : Ideal.map ψ V.ideal.toIdeal = Ideal.span (Set.range MvPowerSeries.X))
  (hH : Ideal.map ψ (Ideal.map (MvPolynomial.aeval f.forms).toRingHom V.ideal.toIdeal) = LinearStudy.equationIdeal H)
  (τ : Fin r → LinearStudy.AmbientRing r c)
  (hτ :
    Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => MvPowerSeries.constantCoeff (τ i)) =
      IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal (LinearStudy.ParameterRing r)) :
  have J := Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (Ideal.Quotient.mk (LinearStudy.equationIdeal H)) (τ i));
  let Q := LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection H ⧸ J;
  have m := Ideal.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk J) (nilradical (LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection H));
  IsArtinianRing Q ∧
    m.IsMaximal ∧
      Submodule.annihilator m = Ideal.span {(Ideal.Quotient.mk J) (LinearStudy.relativeJacobian H)} ∧
        (Ideal.Quotient.mk J) (LinearStudy.relativeJacobian H) ≠ 0

 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]

LinearStudy.finiteModule_exists_generic_homogeneous_basis.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4, u_5} {K : Type u_1} {A : Type u_2}
  {M : Type u_3} [Field K] [CommRing A] [IsDomain A] [AddCommGroup M] [Module K M] [Module A M] [Module.Finite A M]
  (ℳ : ℕ → Submodule K M) [DirectSum.Decomposition ℳ] (F : Type u_4) [Field F] [Algebra A F] [IsFractionRing A F]

  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) :

  (a b : MvPolynomial σ K ⧸ I) (hb : b ∈ LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientPiece I d) :
  (LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientComponent I hI k) (a * b) =
    if d ≤ k then (LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientComponent I hI (k - d)) a * b else 0

LinearStudy.homogeneous_polynomial_low_degree_pairing.{u_1} {K : Type u_1} [Field K] [IsAlgClosed K] [CharZero K]
  {n : ℕ} (P : Fin (n + 1) → MvPolynomial (Fin (n + 1)) K) (e : Fin (n + 1) → ℕ) (he : ∀ (i : Fin (n + 1)), 0 < e i)
  (hh : ∀ (i : Fin (n + 1)), (P i).IsHomogeneous (e i))
  (hreg : RingTheory.Sequence.IsRegular (MvPolynomial (Fin (n + 1)) K) (List.ofFn P))
  (hz : MvPolynomial.zeroLocus K (Ideal.span (Set.range P)) = {0}) :
  let Q := MvPolynomial (Fin (n + 1)) K ⧸ Ideal.span (Set.range P);
  have pi := Ideal.Quotient.mk (Ideal.span (Set.range P));
  have M := fun i j => MvPolynomial.C (↑(e i))⁻¹ * (MvPolynomial.pderiv j) (P i);
  ∃ p,
    p.functional (pi M.det) = 1 ∧
      (∀ (f : MvPolynomial (Fin (n + 1)) K), f.totalDegree < ∑ i, (e i - 1) → p.functional (pi f) = 0) ∧
        ∀ (j : ℕ),
          j ≠ ∑ i, (e i - 1) → ∀ (f : MvPolynomial (Fin (n + 1)) K), f.IsHomogeneous j → p.functional (pi f) = 0

  (I : Ideal (MvPolynomial (Fin (n + 1)) K))
  (hI : Ideal.IsHomogeneous (MvPolynomial.homogeneousSubmodule (Fin (n + 1)) K) I)
  (F : Fin (n + 1) → MvPolynomial (Fin (n + 1)) K) :

      (Ideal.map (MvPolynomial.aeval F).toRingHom I)

  have q := Ideal.Quotient.mk (Ideal.span (Set.range H));

LinearStudy.finiteFlat_residueTensor_jacobian_scalar_socle.{u_1} {K : Type u_1} [Field K] [CharZero K] {r n : ℕ}
  (H : Fin (n + 1) → MvPowerSeries (Fin (n + 1)) (MvPowerSeries (Fin (r + 1)) K))
  (hH : RingTheory.Sequence.IsRegular (MvPowerSeries (Fin (n + 1)) (MvPowerSeries (Fin (r + 1)) K)) (List.ofFn H))
  [Module.Finite (MvPowerSeries (Fin (r + 1)) K)
      (MvPowerSeries (Fin (n + 1)) (MvPowerSeries (Fin (r + 1)) K) ⧸ Ideal.span (Set.range H))]
  [Module.Flat (MvPowerSeries (Fin (r + 1)) K)
      (MvPowerSeries (Fin (n + 1)) (MvPowerSeries (Fin (r + 1)) K) ⧸ Ideal.span (Set.range H))] :

  let A := MvPowerSeries (Fin (n + 1)) B ⧸ Ideal.span (Set.range H);
  let k := B ⧸ IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal B;

  ∀ (x : C),

      ∃ u,
        x =
          u •
            1 ⊗ₜ[B]
              (Ideal.Quotient.mk (Ideal.span (Set.range H))) (Matrix.det fun i j => (MvPowerSeries.pderiv j) (H i))

  let P := Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk I) p;
  let A := Localization.AtPrime P;
  (A ⧸ Ideal.map (algebraMap R A) I) ≃ₐ[R ⧸ I] Localization.AtPrime p

  (r : R) :
  (LinearStudy.localizationQuotientEquiv I p)
      ((Ideal.Quotient.mk (Ideal.map (algebraMap R (Localization.AtPrime (Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk I) p))) I))
        ((algebraMap R (Localization.AtPrime (Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk I) p))) r)) =
    (algebraMap (R ⧸ I) (Localization.AtPrime p)) ((Ideal.Quotient.mk I) r)

  (x : Fin r ⊕ Fin c → K) (G : Fin c → MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K)
  (hG : ∀ (i : Fin c), (MvPolynomial.eval x) (G i) = 0)
  (hJ : IsUnit (Matrix.det fun i j => (MvPolynomial.eval x) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) (G i))))

  (I P Q : Ideal (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K)) [P.IsPrime] [Q.IsPrime] (x : Fin r ⊕ Fin c → K)
  (hP : P = RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval x).toRingHom) (F : Fin r ⊕ Fin c → MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K)
  (hQP : Q = Ideal.comap (MvPolynomial.aeval F).toRingHom P) (hF : Ideal.map (MvPolynomial.aeval F).toRingHom I ≤ I)
  (G : Fin c → MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (hG : ∀ (i : Fin c), (MvPolynomial.eval x) (G i) = 0)

  (hlocal :
    Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I =

  (a : MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) :

LinearStudy.lemma31_local_evaluation_formula {r c : ℕ} (H : Fin c → LinearStudy.AmbientRing r c) (hr : 0 < r)
  (hc : 0 < c) (hH : RingTheory.Sequence.IsRegular (LinearStudy.AmbientRing r c) (List.ofFn H))
  [Module.Finite (LinearStudy.ParameterRing r) (LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection H)]
  [Module.Flat (LinearStudy.ParameterRing r) (LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection H)]
  (q : LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection H →ₐ[LinearStudy.ParameterRing r] LinearStudy.ParameterRing r)
  (hq : RingHom.ker q.toRingHom = nilradical (LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection H))
  (tau : Fin r → LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection H)
  (htau : Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => q (tau i)) = IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal (LinearStudy.ParameterRing r)) :
  have J := Ideal.span (Set.range tau);
  have delta := (Ideal.Quotient.mk J) (LinearStudy.relativeJacobian H);
  ∃ rho,
    rho delta = 1 ∧
      ∀ (h : LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection H),
        rho ((Ideal.Quotient.mk J) h * delta) = MvPowerSeries.constantCoeff (q h)

  (hH : H ≠ 0) (h0 : PowerSeries.constantCoeff H = 0) :

  have delta := (Ideal.Quotient.mk I) (PowerSeries.derivative H);

LinearStudy.original_local_ideal_firstOrder_normal_form.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {K : Type u_1} {α : Type u_2} {β : Type u_3}
  [Field K] [Fintype α] [Fintype β] [DecidableEq α] [DecidableEq β] [Nonempty β] (I : Ideal (MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K))
  (x : α ⊕ β → K) (G : β → MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K)
  (hs :
    Ideal.map
        (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K) (Localization.AtPrime (RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval x).toRingHom))) I =
      Ideal.span
        (Set.range fun i =>
          (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K) (Localization.AtPrime (RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval x).toRingHom)))
            (G i)))
  (h0 : ∀ (i : β), (MvPolynomial.eval x) (G i) = 0)
  (hJ : IsUnit (Matrix.det fun i j => (MvPolynomial.eval x) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) (G i)))) :
  ∃ M,
    ∃ (hM : M.det ≠ 0),
      ∃ H,
        (∀ (i : β), H i - MvPowerSeries.X (Sum.inr i) ∈ IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal (MvPowerSeries (α ⊕ β) K) ^ 2) ∧
          Ideal.map
              ((LinearStudy.formalPolynomialAtPoint (M⁻¹.mulVec x)).comp
                (LinearStudy.polynomialLinearChangeEquiv M hM).toRingEquiv.toRingHom)
              I =
            Ideal.span (Set.range H)

LinearStudy.original_local_ideal_normal_polynomial_generators.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {K : Type u_1} {α : Type u_2}
  {β : Type u_3} [Field K] [Fintype α] [Fintype β] [DecidableEq α] [DecidableEq β] (I : Ideal (MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K))
  (x : α ⊕ β → K) (G : β → MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K)
  (hs :
    Ideal.map
        (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K) (Localization.AtPrime (RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval x).toRingHom))) I =
      Ideal.span
        (Set.range fun i =>
          (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K) (Localization.AtPrime (RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval x).toRingHom)))
            (G i)))
  (h0 : ∀ (i : β), (MvPolynomial.eval x) (G i) = 0)
  (hJ : IsUnit (Matrix.det fun i j => (MvPolynomial.eval x) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) (G i)))) :
  ∃ M,
    ∃ (hM : M.det ≠ 0),
      ∃ H,
        (∀ (i : β), (MvPolynomial.eval (M⁻¹.mulVec x)) (H i) = 0) ∧
          (∀ (i : β) (j : α ⊕ β),
              (MvPolynomial.eval (M⁻¹.mulVec x)) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv j) (H i)) = if j = Sum.inr i then 1 else 0) ∧
            let Q := RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval (M⁻¹.mulVec x)).toRingHom;
            Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K) (Localization.AtPrime Q))
                (Ideal.map (LinearStudy.polynomialLinearChangeEquiv M hM).toRingEquiv.toRingHom I) =
              Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K) (Localization.AtPrime Q)) (H i))

LinearStudy.smooth_point_original_ideal_normal_polynomial_generators.{u_1, u_2} {K : Type u_1} {σ : Type u_2} [Field K]
  [Fintype σ] [DecidableEq σ] (I P : Ideal (MvPolynomial σ K)) [P.IsPrime] (hIP : I ≤ P) (x : σ → K)
  (hP : P = RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval x).toRingHom)
  (hI : Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial σ K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I ≠ ⊥)
  [Algebra.FormallySmooth K
      (Localization.AtPrime P ⧸ Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial σ K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I)] :
  ∃ r c,
    ∃ (_ : 0 < c),
      ∃ e M,
        ∃ (hM : M.det ≠ 0),
          ∃ H,
            r =
                Module.finrank
                  (Localization.AtPrime P ⧸ Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial σ K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I)
                  Ω[Localization.AtPrime P ⧸ Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial σ K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I⁄K] ∧
              (∀ (i : Fin c), (MvPolynomial.eval (M⁻¹.mulVec (x ∘ ⇑e))) (H i) = 0) ∧
                (∀ (i : Fin c) (j : Fin r ⊕ Fin c),
                    (MvPolynomial.eval (M⁻¹.mulVec (x ∘ ⇑e))) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv j) (H i)) =
                      if j = Sum.inr i then 1 else 0) ∧
                  let Q := RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval (M⁻¹.mulVec (x ∘ ⇑e))).toRingHom;
                  Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.AtPrime Q))
                      (Ideal.map
                        ((LinearStudy.polynomialLinearChangeEquiv M hM).toRingEquiv.toRingHom.comp
                          (MvPolynomial.rename ⇑e.symm).toRingHom)
                        I) =
                    Ideal.span
                      (Set.range fun i => (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.AtPrime Q)) (H i))

  [Q.IsPrime] [P.IsPrime] (Q' : Ideal T) (P' : Ideal C) [Q'.IsPrime] [P'.IsPrime] (e : R ≃+* T) (d : A ≃+* C)
  (hQ : Q = Ideal.comap e Q') (hP : P = Ideal.comap d P') (φ : R →+* A) (ψ : T →+* C) (hQP : Q = Ideal.comap φ P)
  (hQ'P' : Q' = Ideal.comap ψ P') (hφ : Ideal.map φ I ≤ J)
  (hψ : Ideal.map ψ (Ideal.map e.toRingHom I) ≤ Ideal.map d.toRingHom J)
  (hd : d.toRingHom.comp φ = ψ.comp e.toRingHom) :

      (LinearStudy.generalPointLocalQuotientPullback I J P Q φ hQP hφ) =

          hψ).comp
      (LinearStudy.pointLocalCoordinateEquiv I Q Q' e hQ).toRingHom

  (hG : ∀ (i : Fin c), (MvPolynomial.eval x) (G i) = 0)
  (hJ : IsUnit (Matrix.det fun i j => (MvPolynomial.eval x) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) (G i)))) (a : K) :
  (LinearStudy.polynomialSmoothFormalMap x G hG hJ) (MvPolynomial.C a) = MvPowerSeries.C (MvPowerSeries.C a)

LinearStudy.polynomialSmoothFormalMap_normal_chain.{u_1} {K : Type u_1} [Field K] {r c : ℕ} [Nonempty (Fin c)]
  (x : Fin r ⊕ Fin c → K) (G : Fin c → MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K)
  (hG : ∀ (i : Fin c), (MvPolynomial.eval x) (G i) = 0)
  (hJ : IsUnit (Matrix.det fun i j => (MvPolynomial.eval x) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) (G i))))
  (P : MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (j : Fin c) :

LinearStudy.polynomial_diagonal_difference.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {R : Type u_1} {A : Type u_2} {B : Type u_3}
  {ι : Type u_4} [CommRing R] [CommRing A] [CommRing B] [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι] (u v : MvPolynomial ι R →+* A)
  (π : A →+* B) (hC : ∀ (c : R), u (MvPolynomial.C c) = v (MvPolynomial.C c))
  (hX : ∀ (i : ι), π (u (MvPolynomial.X i)) = π (v (MvPolynomial.X i))) (p : MvPolynomial ι R) :
  ∃ a,
    u p - v p = ∑ i, a i * (u (MvPolynomial.X i) - v (MvPolynomial.X i)) ∧
      ∀ (i : ι), π (a i) = π (u ((MvPolynomial.pderiv i) p))
'LinearStudy.polynomial_diagonal_difference' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
LinearStudy.polynomial_diagonal_difference_degree.{u_1, u_2} {K : Type u_1} {ι : Type u_2} [Field K] [Fintype ι]
  [DecidableEq ι] (p : MvPolynomial ι K) :
  ∃ a,
    (MvPolynomial.rename Sum.inl) p - (MvPolynomial.rename Sum.inr) p =
        ∑ i, a i * (MvPolynomial.X (Sum.inl i) - MvPolynomial.X (Sum.inr i)) ∧
      (∀ (i : ι), LinearStudy.polynomialDoubleDiagonal (a i) = (MvPolynomial.pderiv i) p) ∧
        (∀ (i : ι), (a i).totalDegree ≤ p.totalDegree - 1) ∧ (p.totalDegree = 0 → ∀ (i : ι), a i = 0)

LinearStudy.polynomialQuotient_nondegenerate_diagonal.{u_3} {F : Type u_3} [Field F] {n : ℕ}
  (P : Fin (n + 1) → MvPolynomial (Fin (n + 1)) F)
  (hP : RingTheory.Sequence.IsRegular (MvPolynomial (Fin (n + 1)) F) (List.ofFn P))
  [Module.Finite F (MvPolynomial (Fin (n + 1)) F ⧸ Ideal.span (Set.range P))] :
  let Q := MvPolynomial (Fin (n + 1)) F ⧸ Ideal.span (Set.range P);
  have pi := Ideal.Quotient.mk (Ideal.span (Set.range P));
  ∃ d,
    LinearStudy.Annihilates (KaehlerDifferential.ideal F Q) d ∧
      ((Algebra.TensorProduct.lmul' F) d = Matrix.det fun i j => pi ((MvPolynomial.pderiv j) (P i))) ∧
        ∀ (q : Q →ₐ[F] F), (LinearStudy.pairingRightReduction q) d ≠ 0

  (P : Fin (n + 1) → MvPolynomial (Fin (n + 1)) F)
  (hP : RingTheory.Sequence.IsRegular (MvPolynomial (Fin (n + 1)) F) (List.ofFn P))

  (J : Ideal (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K)) (F : Fin r ⊕ Fin c → MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K)
  (y : Fin r ⊕ Fin c → K) (hF : ∀ (i : Fin r ⊕ Fin c), F i - MvPolynomial.C (y i) ∈ J)

  (J : Ideal (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K)) (F : Fin r ⊕ Fin c → MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K)

  (J : Ideal (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K)) (F : Fin r ⊕ Fin c → MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K)
  (y : Fin r ⊕ Fin c → K) (hF : ∀ (i : Fin r ⊕ Fin c), F i - MvPolynomial.C (y i) ∈ J)
  (H : Fin c → MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K)
  (htan : ∀ (i : Fin c) (j : Fin r), (MvPolynomial.eval y) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inl j)) (H i)) = 0)
  (hJac : IsUnit (Matrix.det fun i j => (MvPolynomial.eval y) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) (H i)))) :
  Ideal.span
      {(Ideal.Quotient.mk J) (Matrix.det fun i j => (MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) ((MvPolynomial.aeval F) (H i)))} =

  [CommRing R] {r c : ℕ} (J : Ideal (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K))
  (F : Fin r ⊕ Fin c → MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (y : Fin r ⊕ Fin c → K)
  (hF : ∀ (i : Fin r ⊕ Fin c), F i - MvPolynomial.C (y i) ∈ J) (H : Fin c → MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K)

LinearStudy.polynomial_firstOrder_tangents_generate_local_quotient.{u_1, u_3, u_4} {K : Type u_1} [Field K]
  {α : Type u_3} {β : Type u_4} [Finite α] [Finite β] [DecidableEq α] [DecidableEq β]
  (I P : Ideal (MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K)) [P.IsPrime] (hIP : I ≤ P) (x : α ⊕ β → K)
  (hP : P = RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval x).toRingHom) (G : β → MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K)
  (hlocal :
    Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I =
      Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) (G i)))
  (h0 : ∀ (i : β), (MvPolynomial.eval x) (G i) = 0)
  (hD : ∀ (i : β) (j : α ⊕ β), (MvPolynomial.eval x) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv j) (G i)) = if j = Sum.inr i then 1 else 0) :
  let J := Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I;
  Ideal.span
      (Set.range fun i =>
        (Ideal.Quotient.mk J)
          ((algebraMap (MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K) (Localization.AtPrime P))
            (MvPolynomial.X (Sum.inl i) - MvPolynomial.C (x (Sum.inl i))))) =
    IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal (Localization.AtPrime P ⧸ J)

LinearStudy.normalize_polynomial_normal_equations_firstOrder.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {K : Type u_1} [Field K] {α : Type u_2}
  {β : Type u_3} [Fintype α] [Fintype β] [DecidableEq α] [DecidableEq β] (G : β → MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K)
  (x : α ⊕ β → K) (h0 : ∀ (i : β), (MvPolynomial.eval x) (G i) = 0)
  (hT : ∀ (i : β) (j : α), (MvPolynomial.eval x) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inl j)) (G i)) = 0)
  (hJ : IsUnit (Matrix.det fun i j => (MvPolynomial.eval x) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) (G i)))) :
  have J := fun i j => (MvPolynomial.eval x) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) (G i));
  have H := fun i => ∑ j, J⁻¹ i j • G j;
  (∀ (i : β), (MvPolynomial.eval x) (H i) = 0) ∧
    (∀ (i : β) (j : α ⊕ β), (MvPolynomial.eval x) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv j) (H i)) = if j = Sum.inr i then 1 else 0) ∧
      Ideal.span (Set.range H) = Ideal.span (Set.range G)
'LinearStudy.normalize_polynomial_normal_equations_firstOrder' depends on axioms: [propext,
 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]
LinearStudy.polynomial_equations_adapted_normal_generators.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {K : Type u_1} [Field K] {α : Type u_2}
  {β : Type u_3} [Fintype α] [Fintype β] [DecidableEq α] [DecidableEq β] (G : β → MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K)
  (x : α ⊕ β → K) (h0 : ∀ (i : β), (MvPolynomial.eval x) (G i) = 0)
  (hJ : IsUnit (Matrix.det fun i j => (MvPolynomial.eval x) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) (G i)))) :
  ∃ M,
    M.det ≠ 0 ∧
      ∃ H,
        (∀ (i : β), (MvPolynomial.eval (M⁻¹.mulVec x)) (H i) = 0) ∧
          (∀ (i : β) (j : α ⊕ β),
              (MvPolynomial.eval (M⁻¹.mulVec x)) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv j) (H i)) = if j = Sum.inr i then 1 else 0) ∧
            Ideal.span (Set.range H) = Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (LinearStudy.polynomialLinearChange M) (G i))

  (hH : RingTheory.Sequence.IsRegular (MvPowerSeries (Fin (n + 1)) K) (List.ofFn H))

LinearStudy.powerSeries_diagonal_difference.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {R : Type u_1} {A : Type u_2} {B : Type u_3}
  {ι : Type u_4} [CommRing R] [CommRing A] [CommRing B] [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι] [Nonempty ι]
  (u v : MvPowerSeries ι R →+* A) (π : A →+* B) (hC : ∀ (c : R), u (MvPowerSeries.C c) = v (MvPowerSeries.C c))
  (hX : ∀ (i : ι), π (u (MvPowerSeries.X i)) = π (v (MvPowerSeries.X i)))
  (hu : ∀ (i : ι), IsNilpotent (u (MvPowerSeries.X i))) (hv : ∀ (i : ι), IsNilpotent (v (MvPowerSeries.X i)))
  (H : MvPowerSeries ι R) :
  ∃ a,
    u H - v H = ∑ i, a i * (u (MvPowerSeries.X i) - v (MvPowerSeries.X i)) ∧
      ∀ (i : ι), π (a i) = π (u ((MvPowerSeries.pderiv i) H))
'LinearStudy.powerSeries_diagonal_difference' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
LinearStudy.powerSeries_diagonal_jacobian_matrix.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {R : Type u_1} {A : Type u_2} {B : Type u_3}
  {ι : Type u_4} [CommRing R] [CommRing A] [CommRing B] [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι] [Nonempty ι]
  (u v : MvPowerSeries ι R →+* A) (π : A →+* B) (hC : ∀ (c : R), u (MvPowerSeries.C c) = v (MvPowerSeries.C c))
  (hX : ∀ (i : ι), π (u (MvPowerSeries.X i)) = π (v (MvPowerSeries.X i)))
  (hu : ∀ (i : ι), IsNilpotent (u (MvPowerSeries.X i))) (hv : ∀ (i : ι), IsNilpotent (v (MvPowerSeries.X i)))
  (H : ι → MvPowerSeries ι R) (hH : ∀ (i : ι), u (H i) = v (H i)) :
  ∃ M,
    LinearStudy.Annihilates (Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => u (MvPowerSeries.X i) - v (MvPowerSeries.X i))) M.det ∧
      (M.map ⇑π = fun i j => π (u ((MvPowerSeries.pderiv j) (H i)))) ∧
        π M.det = Matrix.det fun i j => π (u ((MvPowerSeries.pderiv j) (H i)))

LinearStudy.powerSeries_coefficientDeterminant_socle.{u_1} {K : Type u_1} [Field K] {n : ℕ}
  (H : Fin (n + 1) → MvPowerSeries (Fin (n + 1)) K)
  (hH : RingTheory.Sequence.IsRegular (MvPowerSeries (Fin (n + 1)) K) (List.ofFn H))

  (hh : ∀ (j : τ), MvPowerSeries.constantCoeff (h j) = 0) (H : τ → MvPowerSeries (σ ⊕ τ) K) :

  (hp0 : (MvPolynomial.eval x) (LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms 0)) ≠ 0) :
  let p0 := LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms 0);
  have p := fun i => LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms i.succ);
  have y := fun i => (MvPolynomial.eval x) (p i) / (MvPolynomial.eval x) p0;
  have P := RingHom.ker (LinearStudy.polynomialAwayPointEvaluation x p0 hp0).toRingHom;
  (V.affinePointIdeal y).IsPrime ∧
    Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk V.affineIdeal) (V.affinePointIdeal y) =
        Ideal.comap (LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p).toRingHom P ∧
      ∃ e,
        0 < e ∧
          Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ) (Localization.Away p0)) V.affineIdeal ^ e ≤
              Ideal.map (LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p).toRingHom V.affineIdeal ∧
            Ideal.map (LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p).toRingHom V.affineIdeal ≤
              Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ) (Localization.Away p0)) V.affineIdeal

 Quot.sound]

LinearStudy.projective_affine_smooth_points_have_common_formal_coordinates.{u_1} {n : ℕ} {ι : Type u_1} [Fintype ι]
  [Nonempty ι] (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hV : V.ideal.toIdeal ≠ ⊥) (x : ι → Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx : ∀ (a : ι), LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint (x a) ∈ V.zeroSet)
  [∀ (a : ι), Algebra.IsSmoothAt ℂ (V.affinePoint (x a) ⋯).asIdeal] :
  ∃ r c,
    ∃ (hc : 0 < c),
      ∃ e M,
        ∃ (hM : M.det ≠ 0),
          ∃ G,
            have C := LinearStudy.polynomialLinearChangeEquiv M hM;
            have y := fun a => M⁻¹.mulVec (x a) ∘ ⇑e;
            have H := fun a i => (MvPolynomial.rename ⇑e.symm) (C (G a i));
            ∃ (hH : ∀ (a : ι) (i : Fin c), (MvPolynomial.eval (y a)) (H a i) = 0) (hJ :
              ∀ (a : ι),
                IsUnit (Matrix.det fun i j => (MvPolynomial.eval (y a)) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) (H a i)))),
              ∀ (a : ι),
                Ideal.map
                    (((LinearStudy.polynomialSmoothFormalMap (y a) (H a) ⋯ ⋯).comp
                          (MvPolynomial.rename ⇑e.symm).toRingHom).comp
                      C.toRingEquiv.toRingHom)
                    V.affineIdeal =
                  Ideal.span (Set.range MvPowerSeries.X)

LinearStudy.projectiveAmbientChart_unramified_at_rational_point {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (x0 : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx0 : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x0 ∈ V.zeroSet)
  (ρ : Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator f V) →ₐ[ℂ] ℂ)
  (hρ :
    have φ := LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap f V hq hf hV x0 hx0;
    LinearStudy.rationalPointPrime ρ ∈
      Algebra.unramifiedLocus (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal)
        (Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator f V))) :
  let p0 := LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms 0);
  let p := fun i => LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms i.succ);
  let J := Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ) (Localization.Away p0)) V.affineIdeal;
  let E := LinearStudy.awayQuotientEquiv V.affineIdeal p0;
  have hI := ⋯;
  let ψ := Ideal.quotientMap J (LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p).toRingHom ⋯;
  let P := Ideal.comap E.toRingEquiv.toRingHom (LinearStudy.rationalPointPrime ρ).asIdeal;
  let Q := Ideal.comap ψ P;
  (LinearStudy.generalPointLocalQuotientPullback V.affineIdeal J (Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk J) P)
      (Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk V.affineIdeal) Q) (LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p).toRingHom ⋯
      hI).FormallyUnramified

LinearStudy.projectiveAmbientFiberPolynomials_regular {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (hq : 0 < f.degree) (y : Fin n → ℂ)
  (havoid :
    ∀ (v : LinearStudy.CoordinateVector n) (hv : v ≠ 0),
      f.onPoints (Projectivization.mk ℂ v hv) = LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y → v 0 ≠ 0) :

          (Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (MvPolynomial.homogeneousComponent (P i).totalDegree) (P i))) =
        {0}

LinearStudy.ProjectiveWholeAmbientFiberConclusion {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (y : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hy : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet) (r : ℕ) : Prop
'LinearStudy.ProjectiveWholeAmbientFiberConclusion' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
LinearStudy.projective_iterates_whole_ambient_fibers_common_geometry {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (hproper : V.ideal.toIdeal ≠ ⊥) (x0 : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx0 : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x0 ∈ V.zeroSet) :
  ∃ r ≤ n,
    ringKrullDim (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal) = ↑r ∧
      Module.finrank (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal) Ω[MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal⁄ℂ] = r ∧
        ∀ (k : ℕ),
          let F := f.iterate k;
          let B := MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal;
          let A := Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator F V);
          have φ := LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap F V ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ x0 hx0;
          ∃ p ∉ V.affineIdeal,
            (∀ (P : PrimeSpectrum A),
                φ ((Ideal.Quotient.mk V.affineIdeal) p) ∉ P.asIdeal →
                  P ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ A ∧
                    PrimeSpectrum.comap φ.toRingHom P ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ B ∧ P ∈ Algebra.unramifiedLocus B A) ∧
              (∃ y, LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet ∧ (MvPolynomial.eval y) p ≠ 0) ∧
                ∀ (y : Fin n → ℂ) (hy : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet),
                  (MvPolynomial.eval y) p ≠ 0 → LinearStudy.ProjectiveWholeAmbientFiberConclusion (f.iterate k) V y hy r
'LinearStudy.projective_iterates_whole_ambient_fibers_common_geometry' depends on axioms: [propext,
 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]

LinearStudy.projectiveAmbientFiberForms_actual_highest_regular {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (hq : 0 < f.degree) (y : Fin n → ℂ)
  (havoid :
    ∀ (v : LinearStudy.CoordinateVector n) (hv : v ≠ 0),
      f.onPoints (Projectivization.mk ℂ v hv) = LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y → v 0 ≠ 0) :

  (∀ (i : Fin n), (P i).totalDegree = f.degree) ∧
    RingTheory.Sequence.IsRegular (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ)
        (List.ofFn fun i => (MvPolynomial.homogeneousComponent (P i).totalDegree) (P i)) ∧
      MvPolynomial.zeroLocus ℂ
          (Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (MvPolynomial.homogeneousComponent (P i).totalDegree) (P i))) =
        {0}

LinearStudy.projectiveAmbientChart_exists_unramified_local_quotient {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (x : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x ∈ V.zeroSet) :
  let B := MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal;
  let A := Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator f V);
  let p0 := LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms 0);
  let p := fun i => LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms i.succ);
  let J := Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ) (Localization.Away p0)) V.affineIdeal;
  let E := LinearStudy.awayQuotientEquiv V.affineIdeal p0;
  have hI := ⋯;
  let ψ := Ideal.quotientMap J (LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p).toRingHom ⋯;
  ∃ ρ,
    LinearStudy.rationalPointPrime ρ ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ A ∧
      LinearStudy.rationalPointPrime (ρ.comp (LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap f V hq hf hV x hx)) ∈
          Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ B ∧
        let P := Ideal.comap E.toRingEquiv.toRingHom (LinearStudy.rationalPointPrime ρ).asIdeal;
        let Q := Ideal.comap ψ P;
        (LinearStudy.generalPointLocalQuotientPullback V.affineIdeal J (Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk J) P)
            (Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk V.affineIdeal) Q) (LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p).toRingHom ⋯
            hI).FormallyUnramified

LinearStudy.projective_actual_centered_fiber_algebra_finite.{u_1} {n : ℕ} {τ : Type u_1} [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ]
  (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (y : Fin n → ℂ)
  (E : MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ≃ₐ[ℂ] MvPolynomial τ ℂ) (b : Fin n ≃ τ) (M : Matrix τ τ ℂ) (hM : M.det ≠ 0) :
  let z := M⁻¹.mulVec (y ∘ ⇑b.symm);
  let p0 := E (LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms 0));
  let p := fun j => E (LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms (b.symm j).succ));
  let pC := fun i => (MvPolynomial.aeval p) (M⁻¹.toMvPolynomial i) - MvPolynomial.C (z i) * p0;
  Module.Finite ℂ (MvPolynomial τ ℂ ⧸ Ideal.span (Set.range pC))

LinearStudy.projective_whole_fiber_centered_rational_local_inputs.{u_1} {n : ℕ} {τ : Type u_1} [Fintype τ]
  [DecidableEq τ] (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n) (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n)
  (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints) (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (x0 : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx0 : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x0 ∈ V.zeroSet) (a : MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ)
  (hgood :
    let B := MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal;
    let A := Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator f V);
    have φ := LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap f V hq hf hV x0 hx0;
    ∀ (P : PrimeSpectrum A),
      φ ((Ideal.Quotient.mk V.affineIdeal) a) ∉ P.asIdeal →
        P ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ A ∧
          PrimeSpectrum.comap φ.toRingHom P ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ B ∧ P ∈ Algebra.unramifiedLocus B A)
  (y : Fin n → ℂ) (hy : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet) (ha : (MvPolynomial.eval y) a ≠ 0)
  (E : MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ≃ₐ[ℂ] MvPolynomial τ ℂ) (b : Fin n ≃ τ) (M : Matrix τ τ ℂ) (hM : M.det ≠ 0) :
  have z := M⁻¹.mulVec (y ∘ ⇑b.symm);
  have p0 := E (LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms 0));
  have p := fun j => E (LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms (b.symm j).succ));
  have pL := fun i => (MvPolynomial.aeval p) (M⁻¹.toMvPolynomial i);
  have pC := fun i => pL i - MvPolynomial.C (z i) * p0;
  let I :=
    Ideal.map (LinearStudy.polynomialTranslation z).toRingEquiv.toRingHom
      (Ideal.map (LinearStudy.polynomialLinearChangeEquiv M hM).toRingEquiv.toRingHom
        (Ideal.map (MvPolynomial.renameEquiv ℂ b).toRingEquiv.toRingHom V.affineIdeal));
  have J := Ideal.map E.toRingEquiv.toRingHom V.affineIdeal;
  let Jaway := Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial τ ℂ) (Localization.Away p0)) J;
  let ψ := LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 pC;
  let Q := RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval 0).toRingHom;
  ∃ e,
    0 < e ∧
      Jaway ^ e ≤ Ideal.map ψ.toRingHom I ∧
        ∃ (hhi : Ideal.map ψ.toRingHom I ≤ Jaway),
          ∀ x ∈ MvPolynomial.zeroLocus ℂ (LinearStudy.projectiveAffineFiberIdeal f V y),
            have x' := fun j => (MvPolynomial.eval x) (E.symm (MvPolynomial.X j));
            ∃ (hp0 : (MvPolynomial.eval x') p0 ≠ 0),
              let P := RingHom.ker (LinearStudy.polynomialAwayPointEvaluation x' p0 hp0).toRingHom;
              ∃ (hQP : Q = Ideal.comap ψ.toRingHom P),
                (LinearStudy.generalPointLocalQuotientPullback I Jaway P Q ψ.toRingHom hQP hhi).FormallyUnramified

LinearStudy.projective_iterates_whole_fibers_centered_normal_targets {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (hproper : V.ideal.toIdeal ≠ ⊥) (x0 : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx0 : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x0 ∈ V.zeroSet) :
  ∃ r ≤ n,
    ringKrullDim (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal) = ↑r ∧
      Module.finrank (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal) Ω[MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal⁄ℂ] = r ∧
        ∀ (k : ℕ),
          let F := f.iterate k;
          let B := MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal;
          let A := Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator F V);
          have φ := LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap F V ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ x0 hx0;
          ∃ p ∉ V.affineIdeal,
            (∀ (P : PrimeSpectrum A),
                φ ((Ideal.Quotient.mk V.affineIdeal) p) ∉ P.asIdeal →
                  P ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ A ∧
                    PrimeSpectrum.comap φ.toRingHom P ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ B ∧ P ∈ Algebra.unramifiedLocus B A) ∧
              (∃ y, LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet ∧ (MvPolynomial.eval y) p ≠ 0) ∧
                ∀ (y : Fin n → ℂ) (hy : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet),
                  (MvPolynomial.eval y) p ≠ 0 →
                    LinearStudy.ProjectiveWholeAmbientFiberConclusion (f.iterate k) V y hy r ∧
                      LinearStudy.ProjectiveLinearTargetParameterConclusion V y hy r ∧
                        LinearStudy.ProjectiveCenteredNormalTargetConclusion V y r

  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (x : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x ∈ V.zeroSet) :
  let B := MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal;
  let A := Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator f V);
  let φ := LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap f V hq hf hV x hx;
  ∃ b,
    b ≠ 0 ∧
      let R := Localization.Away b;
      let S := Localization.Away ((algebraMap B A) b);
      have ψ := Localization.awayMapₐ (Algebra.ofId B A) b;
      Module.Finite R S ∧
        ∃ c,
          c ≠ 0 ∧
            Module.Free (Localization.Away c) (LocalizedModule.Away c S) ∧
              Module.finrank (Localization.Away c) (LocalizedModule.Away c S) = Module.finrank R S

LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap_exists_good_rational_point {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (x : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x ∈ V.zeroSet) :
  let B := MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal;
  let A := Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator f V);
  have φ := LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap f V hq hf hV x hx;
  ∃ ρ,
    LinearStudy.rationalPointPrime ρ ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ A ∧
      LinearStudy.rationalPointPrime (ρ.comp φ) ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ B ∧
        LinearStudy.rationalPointPrime ρ ∈ Algebra.unramifiedLocus B A

LinearStudy.projectiveChartField_exists_hilbert_degree_power {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (x : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x ∈ V.zeroSet) :
  let B := MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal;
  have γ := LinearStudy.projectiveChartFractionMap f V hq hf hV x hx;
  ∃ P,
    P ≠ 0 ∧
      P.natDegree ≤ n ∧
        Module.finrank (↥γ.fieldRange) (FractionRing B) = f.degree ^ P.natDegree ∧
          ∃ K, ∀ N > K, Polynomial.eval (↑N) P = ↑(LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientHilbert V.ideal.toIdeal N)

LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap_exists_good_local_parameters.{u_1} {n : ℕ} {ι : Type u_1}
  (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n) (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree)
  (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints) (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (x : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x ∈ V.zeroSet) :
  let B := MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal;
  let A := Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator f V);
  have φ := LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap f V hq hf hV x hx;
  ∃ ρ,
    LinearStudy.rationalPointPrime ρ ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ A ∧
      LinearStudy.rationalPointPrime (ρ.comp φ) ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ B ∧
        LinearStudy.rationalPointPrime ρ ∈ Algebra.unramifiedLocus B A ∧
          ∀ (t : ι → Localization.AtPrime (LinearStudy.rationalPointPrime (ρ.comp φ)).asIdeal),
            Ideal.span (Set.range t) =
                IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal (Localization.AtPrime (LinearStudy.rationalPointPrime (ρ.comp φ)).asIdeal) →
              Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (LinearStudy.rationalPointLocalMap φ ρ) (t i)) =
                IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal (Localization.AtPrime (LinearStudy.rationalPointPrime ρ).asIdeal)

LinearStudy.projectiveConeFractionField_degree_factor {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (x : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x ∈ V.zeroSet) :
  let A := LinearStudy.CoordinateRing n ⧸ V.ideal.toIdeal;
  let E := LinearStudy.projectiveCoordinateRatioField V;
  have γ := LinearStudy.projectiveCoordinateFractionMap f V hq hf hV;
  have σ := LinearStudy.projectiveCoordinateRatioMap f V hq hf hV x hx;
  Module.finrank (↥γ.fieldRange) (FractionRing A) = f.degree * Module.finrank ↥σ.fieldRange ↥E

LinearStudy.projectiveConeFractionField_exists_hilbert_degree_power {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) :
  let A := LinearStudy.CoordinateRing n ⧸ V.ideal.toIdeal;
  have γ := LinearStudy.projectiveCoordinateFractionMap f V hq hf hV;
  ∃ P C,
    P ≠ 0 ∧
      C ≠ 0 ∧
        P.natDegree ≤ n ∧
          C.natDegree = P.natDegree + 1 ∧
            Module.finrank (↥γ.fieldRange) (FractionRing A) = f.degree ^ C.natDegree ∧
              ∃ K,
                ∀ N > K,
                  Polynomial.eval (↑N) P = ↑(LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientHilbert V.ideal.toIdeal N) ∧
                    Polynomial.eval (↑N) C =
                      ↑(Module.finrank ℂ ↥(LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientFiltration V.ideal.toIdeal N))
'LinearStudy.projectiveConeFractionField_exists_hilbert_degree_power' depends on axioms: [propext,
 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]

  [Finite σ] (I : Ideal (MvPolynomial σ K)) (hI : Ideal.IsHomogeneous (MvPolynomial.homogeneousSubmodule σ K) I)
  (P : Polynomial ℚ) (hP : P ≠ 0)

LinearStudy.projective_coordinate_hilbertPolynomial_growth {n : ℕ} (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) :
  ∃ P C,
    P ≠ 0 ∧
      C ≠ 0 ∧
        P.natDegree ≤ n ∧
          C.natDegree = P.natDegree + 1 ∧
            ∃ N,
              ∀ m > N,
                Polynomial.eval (↑m) P = ↑(LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientHilbert V.ideal.toIdeal m) ∧
                  Polynomial.eval (↑m) C =
                    ↑(Module.finrank ℂ ↥(LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientFiltration V.ideal.toIdeal m))

  [CommRing A] (I : Ideal R) (J : Ideal A) (φ : R →+* A) (hφ : Ideal.map φ I ≤ J) (P P' : Ideal A) (Q Q' : Ideal R)

LinearStudy.projectiveAmbientChart_unramified_at_affine_coordinates {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (x0 : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx0 : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x0 ∈ V.zeroSet)
  (ρ : Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator f V) →ₐ[ℂ] ℂ)
  (hρ :
    have φ := LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap f V hq hf hV x0 hx0;
    LinearStudy.rationalPointPrime ρ ∈
      Algebra.unramifiedLocus (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal)
        (Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator f V)))
  (x : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx :
    (ρ.comp
            (IsScalarTower.toAlgHom ℂ (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal)
              (Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator f V)))).comp
        (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ ℂ V.affineIdeal) =
      MvPolynomial.aeval x)
  (hp0 : (MvPolynomial.eval x) (LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms 0)) ≠ 0) :
  let p0 := LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms 0);
  let p := fun i => LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms i.succ);
  let J := Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ) (Localization.Away p0)) V.affineIdeal;
  let P := RingHom.ker (LinearStudy.polynomialAwayPointEvaluation x p0 hp0).toRingHom;
  let Q := Ideal.comap (LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p).toRingHom P;
  have hI := ⋯;
  (LinearStudy.generalPointLocalQuotientPullback V.affineIdeal J P Q
      (LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p).toRingHom ⋯ hI).FormallyUnramified

LinearStudy.projectiveChart_exists_smooth_unramified_coordinate_point {n : ℕ}
  (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n) (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree)
  (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints) (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (x0 : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx0 : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x0 ∈ V.zeroSet) :
  let p0 := LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms 0);
  let p := fun i => LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms i.succ);
  let J := Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ) (Localization.Away p0)) V.affineIdeal;
  have hI := ⋯;
  ∃ x,
    ∃ (hx : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x ∈ V.zeroSet) (hp0 : (MvPolynomial.eval x) p0 ≠ 0),
      have y := fun i => (MvPolynomial.eval x) (p i) / (MvPolynomial.eval x) p0;
      ∃ (hy : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet),
        Algebra.IsSmoothAt ℂ (V.affinePoint x hx).asIdeal ∧
          Algebra.IsSmoothAt ℂ (V.affinePoint y hy).asIdeal ∧
            let P := RingHom.ker (LinearStudy.polynomialAwayPointEvaluation x p0 hp0).toRingHom;
            let Q := Ideal.comap (LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p).toRingHom P;
            (LinearStudy.generalPointLocalQuotientPullback V.affineIdeal J P Q
                (LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p).toRingHom ⋯ hI).FormallyUnramified

LinearStudy.projectiveCoordinateFiltration_generic_upper {n m : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (b : Fin m → LinearStudy.CoordinateRing n ⧸ V.ideal.toIdeal)
  (d : Fin m → ℕ) (hb : ∀ (i : Fin m), b i ∈ LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientPiece V.ideal.toIdeal (d i))
  (hli :
    let A := LinearStudy.CoordinateRing n ⧸ V.ideal.toIdeal;
    have φ := LinearStudy.projectiveCoordinateDomainMap f V hq hf hV;
    LinearIndependent A b)
  (r : LinearStudy.CoordinateRing n ⧸ V.ideal.toIdeal) (hr : r ≠ 0) (R : ℕ)
  (hrR : r ∈ LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientFiltration V.ideal.toIdeal R)
  (hden :
    ∀ (x : LinearStudy.CoordinateRing n ⧸ V.ideal.toIdeal),
      ∃ c,
        ∑ i, (LinearStudy.projectiveCoordinateDomainMap f V hq hf hV) (c i) * b i =
          (LinearStudy.projectiveCoordinateDomainMap f V hq hf hV) r * x)
  (N : ℕ) :
  Module.finrank ℂ ↥(LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientFiltration V.ideal.toIdeal N) ≤
    m * Module.finrank ℂ ↥(LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientFiltration V.ideal.toIdeal ((f.degree * R + N) / f.degree))
'LinearStudy.projectiveCoordinateFiltration_generic_upper' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
LinearStudy.projective_exists_general_whole_fiber_generic_rank {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (x0 : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx0 : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x0 ∈ V.zeroSet) :
  let B := MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal;
  let A := Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator f V);
  let φ := LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap f V hq hf hV x0 hx0;
  ∃ p ∉ V.affineIdeal,
    (∃ y, LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet ∧ (MvPolynomial.eval y) p ≠ 0) ∧
      ∀ (y : Fin n → ℂ) (hy : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet),
        (MvPolynomial.eval y) p ≠ 0 →
          (f.onPoints ⁻¹' {LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y}).Finite ∧
            (∀ (v : LinearStudy.CoordinateVector n) (hv : v ≠ 0),
                f.onPoints (Projectivization.mk ℂ v hv) = LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y → v 0 ≠ 0) ∧
              have ρ := V.affinePointEvaluation y hy;
              Module.Finite ℂ (TensorProduct B A ℂ) ∧
                IsReduced (TensorProduct B A ℂ) ∧
                  Nat.card ↑(f.onPoints ⁻¹' {LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y}) = Module.finrank B A
'LinearStudy.projective_exists_general_whole_fiber_generic_rank' depends on axioms: [propext,
 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]
LinearStudy.projective_exists_general_whole_reduced_fibers {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (x0 : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx0 : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x0 ∈ V.zeroSet) :
  ∃ p ∉ V.affineIdeal,
    (let B := MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal;
      let A := Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator f V);
      have φ := LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap f V hq hf hV x0 hx0;
      ∀ (P : PrimeSpectrum A),
        φ ((Ideal.Quotient.mk V.affineIdeal) p) ∉ P.asIdeal →
          P ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ A ∧
            PrimeSpectrum.comap φ.toRingHom P ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ B ∧ P ∈ Algebra.unramifiedLocus B A) ∧
      (∃ y, LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet ∧ (MvPolynomial.eval y) p ≠ 0) ∧
        ∀ (y : Fin n → ℂ),
          LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet →
            (MvPolynomial.eval y) p ≠ 0 →
              (f.onPoints ⁻¹' {LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y}).Finite ∧
                (∀ (v : LinearStudy.CoordinateVector n) (hv : v ≠ 0),
                    f.onPoints (Projectivization.mk ℂ v hv) = LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y → v 0 ≠ 0) ∧
                  Module.Finite ℂ (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ LinearStudy.projectiveAffineFiberIdeal f V y) ∧
                    IsReduced (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ LinearStudy.projectiveAffineFiberIdeal f V y) ∧
                      Nat.card ↑(f.onPoints ⁻¹' {LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y}) =
                        Module.finrank ℂ (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ LinearStudy.projectiveAffineFiberIdeal f V y)

  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)

  have γ := LinearStudy.projectiveChartFractionMap f V hq hf hV x0 hx0;
  have φ := LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap f V hq hf hV x0 hx0;
  Module.finrank B A = Module.finrank (↥γ.fieldRange) (FractionRing B)

LinearStudy.projectiveCoordinateDomainMap_generic_growth_comparison {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) :
  let A := LinearStudy.CoordinateRing n ⧸ V.ideal.toIdeal;
  have φ := LinearStudy.projectiveCoordinateDomainMap f V hq hf hV;
  ∃ m B R,
    m = Module.finrank A A ∧
      ∀ (N : ℕ),
        m * Module.finrank ℂ ↥(LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientFiltration V.ideal.toIdeal N) ≤
            Module.finrank ℂ ↥(LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientFiltration V.ideal.toIdeal (f.degree * N + B)) ∧
          Module.finrank ℂ ↥(LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientFiltration V.ideal.toIdeal N) ≤
            m *
              Module.finrank ℂ
                ↥(LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientFiltration V.ideal.toIdeal ((f.degree * R + N) / f.degree))

LinearStudy.projectiveCoordinateDomainMap_rank_eq_growth_power {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)

LinearStudy.projectiveCoordinateDomainMap_exists_hilbert_rank_power {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) :
  let A := LinearStudy.CoordinateRing n ⧸ V.ideal.toIdeal;
  have φ := LinearStudy.projectiveCoordinateDomainMap f V hq hf hV;
  ∃ P C,
    P ≠ 0 ∧
      C ≠ 0 ∧
        P.natDegree ≤ n ∧
          C.natDegree = P.natDegree + 1 ∧
            Module.finrank A A = f.degree ^ C.natDegree ∧
              ∃ K,
                ∀ N > K,
                  Polynomial.eval (↑N) P = ↑(LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientHilbert V.ideal.toIdeal N) ∧
                    Polynomial.eval (↑N) C =
                      ↑(Module.finrank ℂ ↥(LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientFiltration V.ideal.toIdeal N))

  (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n) (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree)
  (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints) (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) :

        (LinearIndependent F fun i => l (b i)) ∧ Submodule.span F (Set.range fun i => l (b i)) = ⊤

LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap_rational_point_coordinates {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (x0 : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx0 : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x0 ∈ V.zeroSet)
  (ρ : Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator f V) →ₐ[ℂ] ℂ) (x : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx :
    (ρ.comp
            (IsScalarTower.toAlgHom ℂ (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal)
              (Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator f V)))).comp
        (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ ℂ V.affineIdeal) =
      MvPolynomial.aeval x) :
  have p0 := LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms 0);
  have p := fun i => LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms i.succ);
  (MvPolynomial.eval x) p0 ≠ 0 ∧
    (ρ.comp (LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap f V hq hf hV x0 hx0)).comp (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ ℂ V.affineIdeal) =
      MvPolynomial.aeval fun i => (MvPolynomial.eval x) (p i) / (MvPolynomial.eval x) p0

 Quot.sound]
LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap_exists_good_affine_coordinates {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (x0 : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx0 : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x0 ∈ V.zeroSet) :
  let B := MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal;
  let A := Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator f V);
  have φ := LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap f V hq hf hV x0 hx0;
  have ι := IsScalarTower.toAlgHom ℂ B A;
  ∃ ρ x,
    ∃ (hx : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x ∈ V.zeroSet) (_ :
      (MvPolynomial.eval x) (LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms 0)) ≠ 0),
      have y := fun i =>
        (MvPolynomial.eval x) (LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms i.succ)) /
          (MvPolynomial.eval x) (LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms 0));
      ∃ (hy : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet),
        Algebra.IsSmoothAt ℂ (V.affinePoint x hx).asIdeal ∧
          Algebra.IsSmoothAt ℂ (V.affinePoint y hy).asIdeal ∧
            LinearStudy.rationalPointPrime ρ ∈ Algebra.unramifiedLocus B A ∧
              (ρ.comp ι).comp (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ ℂ V.affineIdeal) = MvPolynomial.aeval x ∧
                (ρ.comp φ).comp (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ ℂ V.affineIdeal) = MvPolynomial.aeval y

LinearStudy.projectiveCoordinatePiecePullback {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (m : ℕ) :

LinearStudy.projectiveCoordinateCombination_component {n m : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (b c : Fin m → LinearStudy.CoordinateRing n ⧸ V.ideal.toIdeal)
  (d : Fin m → ℕ) (hb : ∀ (i : Fin m), b i ∈ LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientPiece V.ideal.toIdeal (d i)) (k : ℕ) :
  (LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientComponent V.ideal.toIdeal ⋯ k)
      (∑ i, (LinearStudy.projectiveCoordinateDomainMap f V hq hf hV) (c i) * b i) =
    ∑ i,
      (LinearStudy.projectiveCoordinateDomainMap f V hq hf hV)
          (if d i ≤ k ∧ f.degree ∣ k - d i then
            (LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientComponent V.ideal.toIdeal ⋯ ((k - d i) / f.degree)) (c i)
          else 0) *
        b i

LinearStudy.projectiveCoordinateCombination_coefficients_bounded {n m : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)

      LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientFiltration V.ideal.toIdeal N)

LinearStudy.projective_iterates_general_whole_fiber_degree {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (x0 : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx0 : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x0 ∈ V.zeroSet) :
  let B := MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal;
  let L := FractionRing B;
  have γ := LinearStudy.projectiveChartFractionMap f V hq hf hV x0 hx0;
  have D := Module.finrank (↥γ.fieldRange) L;
  0 < D ∧
    ∀ (k : ℕ),
      ∃ p ∉ V.affineIdeal,
        (∃ y, LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet ∧ (MvPolynomial.eval y) p ≠ 0) ∧
          ∀ (y : Fin n → ℂ),
            LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet →
              (MvPolynomial.eval y) p ≠ 0 →
                ((f.iterate k).onPoints ⁻¹' {LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y}).Finite ∧
                  Nat.card ↑((f.iterate k).onPoints ⁻¹' {LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y}) = D ^ k
'LinearStudy.projective_iterates_general_whole_fiber_degree' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
LinearStudy.ProjectiveChartPolynomialParameterConclusion {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (x : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x ∈ V.zeroSet)
  (hp0 : (MvPolynomial.eval x) (LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms 0)) ≠ 0)
  (hy :
    (LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint fun i =>
        (MvPolynomial.eval x) (LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms i.succ)) /
          (MvPolynomial.eval x) (LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms 0))) ∈
      V.zeroSet) :
  Prop

LinearStudy.projectiveChart_exists_actual_polynomial_local_parameters {n : ℕ}
  (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n) (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree)
  (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints) (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (hproper : V.ideal.toIdeal ≠ ⊥)
  (x0 : Fin n → ℂ) (hx0 : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x0 ∈ V.zeroSet) :
  let p0 := LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms 0);
  let p := fun i => LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms i.succ);
  have J := Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ) (Localization.Away p0)) V.affineIdeal;
  ∃ x,
    ∃ (hx : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x ∈ V.zeroSet) (hp0 : (MvPolynomial.eval x) p0 ≠ 0),
      let y := fun i => (MvPolynomial.eval x) (p i) / (MvPolynomial.eval x) p0;
      ∃ (hy : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet),
        Algebra.IsSmoothAt ℂ (V.affinePoint x hx).asIdeal ∧
          Algebra.IsSmoothAt ℂ (V.affinePoint y hy).asIdeal ∧
            LinearStudy.ProjectiveChartPolynomialParameterConclusion f V x hx hp0 hy

 Quot.sound]

LinearStudy.projective_original_whole_fiber_local_socles {n r c : ℕ} (hr : 0 < r) (hc : 0 < c)
  (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n) (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree)
  (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints) (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (x0 : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx0 : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x0 ∈ V.zeroSet) (a : MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ)
  (hgood :
    let B := MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal;
    let A := Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator f V);
    have φ := LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap f V hq hf hV x0 hx0;
    ∀ (P : PrimeSpectrum A),
      φ ((Ideal.Quotient.mk V.affineIdeal) a) ∉ P.asIdeal →
        P ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ A ∧
          PrimeSpectrum.comap φ.toRingHom P ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ B ∧ P ∈ Algebra.unramifiedLocus B A)
  (y : Fin n → ℂ) (hy : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet) (ha : (MvPolynomial.eval y) a ≠ 0)
  (E : MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ≃ₐ[ℂ] MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (b : Fin n ≃ Fin r ⊕ Fin c)
  (M : Matrix (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (hM : M.det ≠ 0)
  (G : ↑(MvPolynomial.zeroLocus ℂ (LinearStudy.projectiveAffineFiberIdeal f V y)) → Fin c → MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ)
  (hG :
    ∀ (x : ↑(MvPolynomial.zeroLocus ℂ (LinearStudy.projectiveAffineFiberIdeal f V y))) (i : Fin c),
      (MvPolynomial.eval fun j => (MvPolynomial.eval ↑x) (E.symm (MvPolynomial.X j))) (E (G x i)) = 0)
  (hJac :
    ∀ (x : ↑(MvPolynomial.zeroLocus ℂ (LinearStudy.projectiveAffineFiberIdeal f V y))),
      IsUnit
        (Matrix.det fun i j =>
          (MvPolynomial.eval fun k => (MvPolynomial.eval ↑x) (E.symm (MvPolynomial.X k)))
            ((MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) (E (G x i)))))
  (hs :
    ∀ (x : ↑(MvPolynomial.zeroLocus ℂ (LinearStudy.projectiveAffineFiberIdeal f V y))),
      let P := RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval ↑x).toRingHom;
      Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime P)) V.affineIdeal =
        Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime P)) (G x i)))
  (H : Fin c → MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (hH0 : ∀ (i : Fin c), (MvPolynomial.eval 0) (H i) = 0)
  (hHD :
    ∀ (i : Fin c) (j : Fin r ⊕ Fin c),
      (MvPolynomial.eval 0) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv j) (H i)) = if j = Sum.inr i then 1 else 0)
  (ht :
    have z := M⁻¹.mulVec (y ∘ ⇑b.symm);
    have I :=
      Ideal.map (LinearStudy.polynomialTranslation z).toRingEquiv.toRingHom
        (Ideal.map (LinearStudy.polynomialLinearChangeEquiv M hM).toRingEquiv.toRingHom
          (Ideal.map (MvPolynomial.renameEquiv ℂ b).toRingEquiv.toRingHom V.affineIdeal));
    let Q := RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval 0).toRingHom;
    I ≤ Q ∧
      Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime Q)) I =
        Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime Q)) (H i))) :
  have z := M⁻¹.mulVec (y ∘ ⇑b.symm);
  have p0 := E (LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms 0));
  have p := fun j => E (LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms (b.symm j).succ));
  have pC := fun i => (MvPolynomial.aeval p) (M⁻¹.toMvPolynomial i) - MvPolynomial.C (z i) * p0;
  let A := MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ ⧸ Ideal.span (Set.range pC);
  have Θ := Matrix.det fun i j => (MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) (pC (Sum.inr i));
  Module.Finite ℂ A ∧
    ∀ (x : ↑(MvPolynomial.zeroLocus ℂ (LinearStudy.projectiveAffineFiberIdeal f V y))),
      have x' := fun j => (MvPolynomial.eval ↑x) (E.symm (MvPolynomial.X j));
      ∃ q,
        (x' = fun i => q ((Ideal.Quotient.mk (Ideal.span (Set.range pC))) (MvPolynomial.X i))) ∧
          have t :=
            (algebraMap A (Localization.AtPrime (RingHom.ker q.toRingHom)))
              ((Ideal.Quotient.mk (Ideal.span (Set.range pC))) Θ);
          Submodule.annihilator (nilradical (Localization.AtPrime (RingHom.ker q.toRingHom))) = Ideal.span {t} ∧ t ≠ 0

LinearStudy.homogeneousComponent_aeval_positive_degree_all.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {K : Type u_1} {ι : Type u_2} {κ : Type u_3}
  [Field K] (P : κ → MvPolynomial ι K) (q : ℕ) (hq : 0 < q) (hP : ∀ (i : κ), (P i).IsHomogeneous q)

LinearStudy.projectiveCoordinateDomainMap_component_all {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (k : ℕ) (a : LinearStudy.CoordinateRing n ⧸ V.ideal.toIdeal) :
  (LinearStudy.homogeneousQuotientComponent V.ideal.toIdeal ⋯ k)
      ((LinearStudy.projectiveCoordinateDomainMap f V hq hf hV) a) =

  (hVf : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (hVg : g.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet)

  (hVc : (f.comp g).onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (x0 : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx0 : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x0 ∈ V.zeroSet) :
  LinearStudy.projectiveChartFractionMap (f.comp g) V hcq hc hVc x0 hx0 =
    (LinearStudy.projectiveChartFractionMap g V hgq hg hVg x0 hx0).comp
      (LinearStudy.projectiveChartFractionMap f V hfq hf hVf x0 hx0)

  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (x : ι → Fin n → ℂ)

LinearStudy.projective_smooth_points_common_coordinates_actual_dimension.{u_1} {n : ℕ} {ι : Type u_1} [Fintype ι]
  [Nonempty ι] (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hproper : V.ideal.toIdeal ≠ ⊥) (x : ι → Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx : ∀ (a : ι), LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint (x a) ∈ V.zeroSet)
  [∀ (a : ι), Algebra.IsSmoothAt ℂ (V.affinePoint (x a) ⋯).asIdeal] :
  ∃ r c,
    ringKrullDim (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal) = ↑r ∧
      Module.finrank (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal) Ω[MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal⁄ℂ] = r ∧
        c = n - r ∧
          ∃ (hc : 0 < c),
            ∃ e M,
              ∃ (hM : M.det ≠ 0),
                ∃ G,
                  have C := LinearStudy.polynomialLinearChangeEquiv M hM;
                  have y := fun a => M⁻¹.mulVec (x a) ∘ ⇑e;
                  have H := fun a i => (MvPolynomial.rename ⇑e.symm) (C (G a i));
                  ∃ (hH : ∀ (a : ι) (i : Fin c), (MvPolynomial.eval (y a)) (H a i) = 0) (hJ :
                    ∀ (a : ι),
                      IsUnit
                        (Matrix.det fun i j => (MvPolynomial.eval (y a)) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) (H a i)))),
                    (∀ (a : ι),
                        Ideal.map
                            (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ)
                              (Localization.AtPrime
                                (Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk V.affineIdeal) (V.affinePoint (x a) ⋯).asIdeal)))
                            V.affineIdeal =
                          Ideal.span
                            (Set.range fun i =>
                              (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ)
                                  (Localization.AtPrime
                                    (Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk V.affineIdeal) (V.affinePoint (x a) ⋯).asIdeal)))
                                (G a i))) ∧
                      ∀ (a : ι),
                        Ideal.map
                            (((LinearStudy.polynomialSmoothFormalMap (y a) (H a) ⋯ ⋯).comp
                                  (MvPolynomial.rename ⇑e.symm).toRingHom).comp
                              C.toRingEquiv.toRingHom)
                            V.affineIdeal =
                          Ideal.span (Set.range MvPowerSeries.X)
'LinearStudy.projective_smooth_points_common_coordinates_actual_dimension' depends on axioms: [propext,
 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]

LinearStudy.projective_iterates_whole_ambient_fibers_linear_targets {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (hproper : V.ideal.toIdeal ≠ ⊥) (x0 : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx0 : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x0 ∈ V.zeroSet) :
  ∃ r ≤ n,
    ringKrullDim (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal) = ↑r ∧
      Module.finrank (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal) Ω[MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal⁄ℂ] = r ∧
        ∀ (k : ℕ),
          let F := f.iterate k;
          let B := MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal;
          let A := Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator F V);
          have φ := LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap F V ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ x0 hx0;
          ∃ p ∉ V.affineIdeal,
            (∀ (P : PrimeSpectrum A),
                φ ((Ideal.Quotient.mk V.affineIdeal) p) ∉ P.asIdeal →
                  P ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ A ∧
                    PrimeSpectrum.comap φ.toRingHom P ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ B ∧ P ∈ Algebra.unramifiedLocus B A) ∧
              (∃ y, LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet ∧ (MvPolynomial.eval y) p ≠ 0) ∧
                ∀ (y : Fin n → ℂ) (hy : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet),
                  (MvPolynomial.eval y) p ≠ 0 →
                    LinearStudy.ProjectiveWholeAmbientFiberConclusion (f.iterate k) V y hy r ∧
                      LinearStudy.ProjectiveLinearTargetParameterConclusion V y hy r
'LinearStudy.projective_iterates_whole_ambient_fibers_linear_targets' depends on axioms: [propext,
 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]
LinearStudy.projective_smooth_local_pullback_structure {n r c : ℕ} [Nonempty (Fin c)]
  (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n) (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hf : 1 < f.degree)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (e : Fin (n + 1) ≃ Fin r ⊕ Fin c) (hr : 0 < r) (hc : 0 < c)
  (P Q : Ideal (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ)) [P.IsPrime] [Q.IsPrime] (x : Fin r ⊕ Fin c → ℂ)
  (hP : P = RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval x).toRingHom) (G H : Fin c → MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ)
  (hG : ∀ (i : Fin c), (MvPolynomial.eval x) (G i) = 0)
  (hJ : IsUnit (Matrix.det fun i j => (MvPolynomial.eval x) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) (G i))))
  (hQ :
    Q =
      RingHom.ker
        (MvPolynomial.aeval fun i =>
            (MvPolynomial.eval x) ((MvPolynomial.renameEquiv ℂ e) (f.forms (e.symm i)))).toRingHom)
  (hsource :
    Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime P))
        (Ideal.map (MvPolynomial.renameEquiv ℂ e).toRingEquiv.toRingHom V.ideal.toIdeal) =
      Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime P)) (G i)))
  (htarget :
    Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime Q))
        (Ideal.map (MvPolynomial.renameEquiv ℂ e).toRingEquiv.toRingHom V.ideal.toIdeal) =
      Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime Q)) (H i))) :
  have F := fun i => (MvPolynomial.renameEquiv ℂ e) (f.forms (e.symm i));
  have L := LinearStudy.smoothPullbackEquations x G hG hJ F H;
  RingTheory.Sequence.IsRegular (LinearStudy.AmbientRing r c) (List.ofFn L) ∧
    Module.Finite (LinearStudy.ParameterRing r) (LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection L) ∧
      Module.Free (LinearStudy.ParameterRing r) (LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection L)
'LinearStudy.projective_smooth_local_pullback_structure' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
LinearStudy.projective_smooth_local_parameter_jacobian_socle {n r c : ℕ} [Nonempty (Fin c)]
  (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n) (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hf : 1 < f.degree)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (e : Fin (n + 1) ≃ Fin r ⊕ Fin c) (hr : 0 < r) (hc : 0 < c)
  (P Q : Ideal (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ)) [P.IsPrime] [Q.IsPrime] (x : Fin r ⊕ Fin c → ℂ)
  (hP : P = RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval x).toRingHom) (G H : Fin c → MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ)
  (hG : ∀ (i : Fin c), (MvPolynomial.eval x) (G i) = 0)
  (hJ : IsUnit (Matrix.det fun i j => (MvPolynomial.eval x) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) (G i))))
  (hQ :
    Q =
      RingHom.ker
        (MvPolynomial.aeval fun i =>
            (MvPolynomial.eval x) ((MvPolynomial.renameEquiv ℂ e) (f.forms (e.symm i)))).toRingHom)
  (hsource :
    Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime P))
        (Ideal.map (MvPolynomial.renameEquiv ℂ e).toRingEquiv.toRingHom V.ideal.toIdeal) =
      Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime P)) (G i)))
  (htarget :
    Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime Q))
        (Ideal.map (MvPolynomial.renameEquiv ℂ e).toRingEquiv.toRingHom V.ideal.toIdeal) =
      Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime Q)) (H i)))
  (τ : Fin r → LinearStudy.AmbientRing r c)
  (hτ :
    Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => MvPowerSeries.constantCoeff (τ i)) =
      IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal (LinearStudy.ParameterRing r)) :
  have F := fun i => (MvPolynomial.renameEquiv ℂ e) (f.forms (e.symm i));
  have L := LinearStudy.smoothPullbackEquations x G hG hJ F H;
  have J := Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (Ideal.Quotient.mk (LinearStudy.equationIdeal L)) (τ i));
  let A := LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection L ⧸ J;
  have m := Ideal.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk J) (nilradical (LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection L));
  IsArtinianRing A ∧
    m.IsMaximal ∧
      Submodule.annihilator m = Ideal.span {(Ideal.Quotient.mk J) (LinearStudy.relativeJacobian L)} ∧
        (Ideal.Quotient.mk J) (LinearStudy.relativeJacobian L) ≠ 0

 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]
LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations.exists_smooth_affine_point_avoiding {n : ℕ}
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (x0 : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx0 : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x0 ∈ V.zeroSet) (H : MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ) (hH : H ∉ V.affineIdeal) :
  ∃ x,
    ∃ (hx : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x ∈ V.zeroSet),
      (MvPolynomial.eval x) H ≠ 0 ∧ Algebra.IsSmoothAt ℂ (V.affinePoint x hx).asIdeal

LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations.smoothPoint_polynomial_parameters {n : ℕ}
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hproper : V.ideal.toIdeal ≠ ⊥) (y : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hy : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet) (hs : Algebra.IsSmoothAt ℂ (V.affinePoint y hy).asIdeal) :
  let P := Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk V.affineIdeal) (V.affinePoint y hy).asIdeal;
  have J := Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime P)) V.affineIdeal;
  ∃ r a,
    r = Module.finrank (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal) Ω[MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal⁄ℂ] ∧
      Ideal.span
          (Set.range fun i =>
            (Ideal.Quotient.mk J) ((algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime P)) (a i))) =
        Ideal.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk J) (Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime P)) P)

LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap_exists_target_good_loci {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (x : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x ∈ V.zeroSet) :
  let B := MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal;
  let A := Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator f V);
  have φ := LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap f V hq hf hV x hx;
  ∃ b,
    b ≠ 0 ∧
      φ b ≠ 0 ∧
        ∀ (P : PrimeSpectrum A),
          φ b ∉ P.asIdeal →
            P ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ A ∧
              PrimeSpectrum.comap φ.toRingHom P ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ B ∧ P ∈ Algebra.unramifiedLocus B A

  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (x : Fin n → ℂ)

            ∀ (P : PrimeSpectrum (Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator f V))),
              (LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap f V hq hf hV x hx) b ∉ P.asIdeal → a ∉ P.asIdeal

LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations.smoothTarget_actual_normal_coordinates {n : ℕ}
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hproper : V.ideal.toIdeal ≠ ⊥) (y : Fin n → ℂ)

LinearStudy.projective_iterates_whole_ambient_fibers_normal_targets {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (hproper : V.ideal.toIdeal ≠ ⊥) (x0 : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx0 : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x0 ∈ V.zeroSet) :
  ∃ r ≤ n,
    ringKrullDim (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal) = ↑r ∧
      Module.finrank (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal) Ω[MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal⁄ℂ] = r ∧
        ∀ (k : ℕ),
          let F := f.iterate k;
          let B := MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal;
          let A := Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator F V);
          have φ := LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap F V ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ x0 hx0;
          ∃ p ∉ V.affineIdeal,
            (∀ (P : PrimeSpectrum A),
                φ ((Ideal.Quotient.mk V.affineIdeal) p) ∉ P.asIdeal →
                  P ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ A ∧
                    PrimeSpectrum.comap φ.toRingHom P ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ B ∧ P ∈ Algebra.unramifiedLocus B A) ∧
              (∃ y, LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet ∧ (MvPolynomial.eval y) p ≠ 0) ∧
                ∀ (y : Fin n → ℂ) (hy : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet),
                  (MvPolynomial.eval y) p ≠ 0 →
                    LinearStudy.ProjectiveWholeAmbientFiberConclusion (f.iterate k) V y hy r ∧
                      LinearStudy.ProjectiveLinearTargetParameterConclusion V y hy r ∧
                        LinearStudy.ProjectiveTargetNormalCoordinateConclusion V y r
