LinearStudy.projective_whole_fibers_and_smooth_parameters_same_dimension {n : ℕ}
  (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n) (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree)
  (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints) (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (hproper : V.ideal.toIdeal ≠ ⊥)
  (x0 : Fin n → ℂ) (hx0 : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x0 ∈ V.zeroSet) :
  let B := MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal;
  ∃ r ≤ n,
    ringKrullDim B = ↑r ∧
      Module.finrank B Ω[B⁄ℂ] = r ∧
        (∀ (y : Fin n → ℂ) (hy : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet),
            Algebra.IsSmoothAt ℂ (V.affinePoint y hy).asIdeal →
              let Q := Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk V.affineIdeal) (V.affinePoint y hy).asIdeal;
              have J := Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime Q)) V.affineIdeal;
              ∃ a,
                Ideal.span
                    (Set.range fun i =>
                      (Ideal.Quotient.mk J) ((algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime Q)) (a i))) =
                  Ideal.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk J)
                    (Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime Q)) Q)) ∧
          ∀ (k : ℕ),
            ∃ p ∉ V.affineIdeal,
              (∃ y, LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet ∧ (MvPolynomial.eval y) p ≠ 0) ∧
                ∀ (y : Fin n → ℂ),
                  LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet →
                    (MvPolynomial.eval y) p ≠ 0 →
                      ((f.iterate k).onPoints ⁻¹' {LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y}).Finite ∧
                        Nat.card ↑((f.iterate k).onPoints ⁻¹' {LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y}) =
                          (f.degree ^ k) ^ r
'LinearStudy.projective_whole_fibers_and_smooth_parameters_same_dimension' depends on axioms: [propext,
 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]
LinearStudy.projective_iterates_whole_good_fibers_same_dimension {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (hproper : V.ideal.toIdeal ≠ ⊥) (x0 : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx0 : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x0 ∈ V.zeroSet) :
  let B := MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal;
  ∃ r ≤ n,
    ringKrullDim B = ↑r ∧
      Module.finrank B Ω[B⁄ℂ] = r ∧
        (∀ (y : Fin n → ℂ) (hy : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet),
            Algebra.IsSmoothAt ℂ (V.affinePoint y hy).asIdeal →
              let Q := Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk V.affineIdeal) (V.affinePoint y hy).asIdeal;
              have J := Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime Q)) V.affineIdeal;
              ∃ a,
                Ideal.span
                    (Set.range fun i =>
                      (Ideal.Quotient.mk J) ((algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime Q)) (a i))) =
                  Ideal.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk J)
                    (Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime Q)) Q)) ∧
          ∀ (k : ℕ),
            let F := f.iterate k;
            let A := Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator F V);
            have φ := LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap F V ⋯ ⋯ ⋯ x0 hx0;
            ∃ p ∉ V.affineIdeal,
              (∀ (P : PrimeSpectrum A),
                  φ ((Ideal.Quotient.mk V.affineIdeal) p) ∉ P.asIdeal →
                    P ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ A ∧
                      PrimeSpectrum.comap φ.toRingHom P ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ B ∧ P ∈ Algebra.unramifiedLocus B A) ∧
                (∃ y, LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet ∧ (MvPolynomial.eval y) p ≠ 0) ∧
                  ∀ (y : Fin n → ℂ),
                    LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y ∈ V.zeroSet →
                      (MvPolynomial.eval y) p ≠ 0 →
                        (F.onPoints ⁻¹' {LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y}).Finite ∧
                          (∀ (v : LinearStudy.CoordinateVector n) (hv : v ≠ 0),
                              F.onPoints (Projectivization.mk ℂ v hv) = LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y →
                                v 0 ≠ 0) ∧
                            Module.Finite ℂ (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ LinearStudy.projectiveAffineFiberIdeal F V y) ∧
                              IsReduced (MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ LinearStudy.projectiveAffineFiberIdeal F V y) ∧
                                Nat.card ↑(F.onPoints ⁻¹' {LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint y}) =
                                  (f.degree ^ k) ^ r
'LinearStudy.projective_iterates_whole_good_fibers_same_dimension' depends on axioms: [propext,
 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]
LinearStudy.projective_exists_whole_fiber_good_target_point {n : ℕ} (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n)
  (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n) (hq : 0 < f.degree) (hf : Function.Surjective f.onPoints)
  (hV : f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet) (x0 : Fin n → ℂ)
  (hx0 : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint x0 ∈ V.zeroSet) :
  let B := MvPolynomial (Fin n) ℂ ⧸ V.affineIdeal;
  let A := Localization.Away (LinearStudy.projectiveChartDenominator f V);
  have φ := LinearStudy.projectiveChartOpenMap f V hq hf hV x0 hx0;
  have ι := IsScalarTower.toAlgHom ℂ B A;
  ∃ w,
    ∃ (hw : w ≠ 0),
      w ∈ MvPolynomial.zeroLocus ℂ V.ideal.toIdeal ∧
        (f.onPoints ⁻¹' {Projectivization.mk ℂ w hw}).Finite ∧
          (∀ (v : LinearStudy.CoordinateVector n) (hv : v ≠ 0),
              f.onPoints (Projectivization.mk ℂ v hv) = Projectivization.mk ℂ w hw → v 0 ≠ 0) ∧
            ∀ (z : Fin n → ℂ) (hz : LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint z ∈ V.zeroSet),
              f.onPoints (LinearStudy.normalizedProjectivePoint z) = Projectivization.mk ℂ w hw →
                ∃ (hp0 : (MvPolynomial.eval z) (LinearStudy.affineChartPolynomialMap (f.forms 0)) ≠ 0),
                  have ρ := LinearStudy.projectiveChartPointEvaluation f V z hz hp0;
                  LinearStudy.rationalPointPrime ρ ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ A ∧
                    LinearStudy.rationalPointPrime (ρ.comp φ) ∈ Algebra.smoothLocus ℂ B ∧
                      LinearStudy.rationalPointPrime ρ ∈ Algebra.unramifiedLocus B A ∧
                        (ρ.comp ι).comp (Ideal.Quotient.mkₐ ℂ V.affineIdeal) = MvPolynomial.aeval z

LinearStudy.quotientLocalPullback_comparison.{u_1, u_2} {R : Type u_1} {A : Type u_2} [CommRing R] [CommRing A]
  (I : Ideal R) (J : Ideal A) (φ : R →+* A) (hφ : Ideal.map φ I ≤ J) (P : Ideal (A ⧸ J)) [P.IsPrime] :
  let ψ := Ideal.quotientMap J φ ⋯;
  let Q := Ideal.comap ψ P;
  let p := Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk J) P;
  let q := Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk I) Q;
  (LinearStudy.localizationQuotientEquiv J P).toRingEquiv.toRingHom.comp
      (LinearStudy.generalPointLocalQuotientPullback I J p q φ ⋯ hφ) =
    (Localization.localRingHom Q P ψ ⋯).comp (LinearStudy.localizationQuotientEquiv I Q).toRingEquiv.toRingHom
'LinearStudy.quotientLocalPullback_comparison' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
LinearStudy.quotientLocalPullback_formallyUnramified.{u_1, u_2} {R : Type u_1} {A : Type u_2} [CommRing R] [CommRing A]
  (I : Ideal R) (J : Ideal A) (φ : R →+* A) (hφ : Ideal.map φ I ≤ J) (P : Ideal (A ⧸ J)) [P.IsPrime]
  (hP :
    have ψ := Ideal.quotientMap J φ ⋯;
    (Localization.localRingHom (Ideal.comap ψ P) P ψ ⋯).FormallyUnramified) :
  let ψ := Ideal.quotientMap J φ ⋯;
  let Q := Ideal.comap ψ P;
  let p := Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk J) P;
  let q := Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk I) Q;
  (LinearStudy.generalPointLocalQuotientPullback I J p q φ ⋯ hφ).FormallyUnramified

LinearStudy.rational_linear_normal_common_theta_formal_socle {r c : ℕ} [Nonempty (Fin r)] [Nonempty (Fin c)]
  (x : Fin r ⊕ Fin c → ℂ) (p0 : MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (hp0 : (MvPolynomial.eval x) p0 ≠ 0)
  (p : Fin r ⊕ Fin c → MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (hp : ∀ (i : Fin r ⊕ Fin c), (MvPolynomial.eval x) (p i) = 0)
  (I J : Ideal (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ)) (P : Ideal (Localization.Away p0))
  (Q : Ideal (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ)) [P.IsPrime] [Q.IsPrime]
  (hP : P = RingHom.ker (LinearStudy.polynomialAwayPointEvaluation x p0 hp0).toRingHom)
  (hQ : Q = RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval 0).toRingHom)
  (hQP : Q = Ideal.comap (LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p).toRingHom P) (hIQ : I ≤ Q)
  (G H : Fin c → MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (hG : ∀ (i : Fin c), (MvPolynomial.eval x) (G i) = 0)
  (hJac : IsUnit (Matrix.det fun i j => (MvPolynomial.eval x) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) (G i))))
  (hsource :
    Ideal.map
        (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ)
          (Localization.AtPrime (Ideal.comap (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (Localization.Away p0)) P)))
        J =
      Ideal.span
        (Set.range fun i =>
          (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ)
              (Localization.AtPrime
                (Ideal.comap (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (Localization.Away p0)) P)))
            (G i)))
  (htarget :
    Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime Q)) I =
      Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime Q)) (H i)))
  (hH0 : ∀ (i : Fin c), (MvPolynomial.eval 0) (H i) = 0)
  (hHD :
    ∀ (i : Fin c) (j : Fin r ⊕ Fin c),
      (MvPolynomial.eval 0) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv j) (H i)) = if j = Sum.inr i then 1 else 0)
  (e : ℕ) (he : 0 < e)
  (hlo :
    Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (Localization.Away p0)) J ^ e ≤
      Ideal.map (LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p).toRingHom I)
  (hhi :
    Ideal.map (LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p).toRingHom I ≤
      Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (Localization.Away p0)) J)
  (hunram :
    (LinearStudy.generalPointLocalQuotientPullback I
        (Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (Localization.Away p0)) J) P Q
        (LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p).toRingHom hQP hhi).FormallyUnramified)
  (hr : 0 < r) (hc : 0 < c) :
  have ψ := LinearStudy.polynomialSmoothFormalMap x G hG hJac;
  have Jf := Ideal.map ψ (Ideal.span (Set.range p));
  have Θ := Matrix.det fun i j => (MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) (p (Sum.inr i));
  Submodule.annihilator (nilradical (LinearStudy.AmbientRing r c ⧸ Jf)) = Ideal.span {(Ideal.Quotient.mk Jf) (ψ Θ)} ∧
    (Ideal.Quotient.mk Jf) (ψ Θ) ≠ 0

LinearStudy.rational_reindexed_centered_actual_local_map.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {K : Type u_1} {σ : Type u_2} {τ : Type u_3}
  [Field K] [Fintype τ] [DecidableEq τ] (E : MvPolynomial σ K ≃ₐ[K] MvPolynomial τ K) (b : σ ≃ τ) (M : Matrix τ τ K)
  (hM : M.det ≠ 0) (z : τ → K) (p0 : MvPolynomial σ K) (p : σ → MvPolynomial σ K) (I J Q : Ideal (MvPolynomial σ K))
  [Q.IsPrime] (P : Ideal (Localization.Away p0)) [P.IsPrime]
  (hQP : Q = Ideal.comap (LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p).toRingHom P)
  (hφ :
    Ideal.map (LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p).toRingHom I ≤
      Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial σ K) (Localization.Away p0)) J)
  (h :
    (LinearStudy.generalPointLocalQuotientPullback I
        (Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial σ K) (Localization.Away p0)) J) P Q
        (LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p).toRingHom hQP hφ).FormallyUnramified) :
  have E0 := MvPolynomial.renameEquiv K b;
  have EL := LinearStudy.polynomialLinearChangeEquiv M hM;
  have EC := LinearStudy.polynomialTranslation z;
  have d := LinearStudy.polynomialAwayCoordinateEquiv E p0;
  let P' := Ideal.comap d.symm.toRingEquiv.toRingHom P;
  let Q' :=
    Ideal.comap EC.symm.toRingEquiv.toRingHom
      (Ideal.comap EL.symm.toRingEquiv.toRingHom (Ideal.comap E0.symm.toRingEquiv.toRingHom Q));
  have p' := fun j => E (p (b.symm j));
  have pL := fun i => (MvPolynomial.aeval p') (M⁻¹.toMvPolynomial i);
  have pC := fun i => pL i - MvPolynomial.C (z i) * E p0;
  let ψ := LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap (E p0) pC;
  let I' :=
    Ideal.map EC.toRingEquiv.toRingHom (Ideal.map EL.toRingEquiv.toRingHom (Ideal.map E0.toRingEquiv.toRingHom I));
  let J' := Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial τ K) (Localization.Away (E p0))) (Ideal.map E.toRingEquiv.toRingHom J);
  ∃ (hQP' : Q' = Ideal.comap ψ.toRingHom P') (hψ : Ideal.map ψ.toRingHom I' ≤ J'),
    (LinearStudy.generalPointLocalQuotientPullback I' J' P' Q' ψ.toRingHom hQP' hψ).FormallyUnramified

LinearStudy.rational_source_coordinates_target_reindex_unramified.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {K : Type u_1} {σ : Type u_2}

  (p : σ → MvPolynomial σ K) (I J Q : Ideal (MvPolynomial σ K)) [Q.IsPrime] (P : Ideal (Localization.Away p0))
  [P.IsPrime] (hQP : Q = Ideal.comap (LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p).toRingHom P)
  (hφ :
    Ideal.map (LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p).toRingHom I ≤
      Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial σ K) (Localization.Away p0)) J)
  (h :
    (LinearStudy.generalPointLocalQuotientPullback I
        (Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial σ K) (Localization.Away p0)) J) P Q
        (LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p).toRingHom hQP hφ).FormallyUnramified) :

  have d := LinearStudy.polynomialAwayCoordinateEquiv e p0;

  let P' := Ideal.comap d.symm.toRingEquiv.toRingHom P;
  let ψ := LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap (e p0) fun j => e (p (b.symm j));

LinearStudy.rational_smooth_unramified_constructs_jacobian_socle {r c : ℕ} [Nonempty (Fin r)] [Nonempty (Fin c)]

    Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk I) p' =

  (hφ :
    Ideal.map (LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p).toRingHom I ≤
      Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (Localization.Away p0)) J)

        (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ)

      (Localization.AtPrime P ⧸
        Ideal.map (algebraMap (Localization.Away p0) (Localization.AtPrime P))
          (Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.Away p0)) J)))
  (hunram :
    Algebra.FormallyUnramified
      (Localization.AtPrime Q ⧸ Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.AtPrime Q)) I)
      (Localization.AtPrime P ⧸
        Ideal.map (algebraMap (Localization.Away p0) (Localization.AtPrime P))
          (Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.Away p0)) J)))
  (a : Fin r → MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K)
  (ha :
    Ideal.span
        (Set.range fun i =>
          (Ideal.Quotient.mk (Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.AtPrime Q)) I))
            ((algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.AtPrime Q)) (a i))) =
      Ideal.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.AtPrime Q)) I))
        (Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.AtPrime Q)) Q)) :
  have T := fun i =>
    (LinearStudy.polynomialAwaySmoothReducedMap x p0 hp0 G hG hJ) ((LinearStudy.rationalPolynomialChartMap p0 p) (a i));
  Ideal.span (Set.range T) = IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal (MvPowerSeries (Fin r) K) ∧
    IsUnit (Matrix.det fun i j => MvPowerSeries.constantCoeff ((MvPowerSeries.pderiv j) (T i)))

  {ι : Type u_3} [Field K] [Infinite K] [Fintype σ] [DecidableEq σ] [Fintype ι] (I : Ideal (MvPolynomial σ K))
  (P : ι → Ideal (MvPolynomial σ K)) [∀ (a : ι), (P a).IsPrime] (x : ι → σ → K) (hIP : ∀ (a : ι), I ≤ P a)
  (hP : ∀ (a : ι), P a = RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval (x a)).toRingHom)

        (Localization.AtPrime (P a) ⧸ Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial σ K) (Localization.AtPrime (P a))) I)]

LinearStudy.smooth_points_have_common_formal_normal_coordinates.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {K : Type u_1} {σ : Type u_2}
  {ι : Type u_3} [Field K] [Infinite K] [Fintype σ] [DecidableEq σ] [Fintype ι] (I : Ideal (MvPolynomial σ K))
  (P : ι → Ideal (MvPolynomial σ K)) [∀ (a : ι), (P a).IsPrime] (x : ι → σ → K) (hIP : ∀ (a : ι), I ≤ P a)
  (hP : ∀ (a : ι), P a = RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval (x a)).toRingHom)
  [∀ (a : ι),
      Algebra.FormallySmooth K
        (Localization.AtPrime (P a) ⧸ Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial σ K) (Localization.AtPrime (P a))) I)]
  {c : ℕ} [Nonempty (Fin c)] (ν : Fin c → σ) (hν : Function.Injective ν)
  (hc :
    ∀ (a : ι),
      Module.finrank
          (Localization.AtPrime (P a) ⧸ Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial σ K) (Localization.AtPrime (P a))) I)
          (LinearStudy.polynomialLocalQuotientExtension I (P a)).Cotangent =
        c) :
  ∃ r e M,
    ∃ (hM : M.det ≠ 0),
      ∃ G,
        have C := LinearStudy.polynomialLinearChangeEquiv M hM;
        have y := fun a => M⁻¹.mulVec (x a) ∘ ⇑e;
        have H := fun a i => (MvPolynomial.rename ⇑e.symm) (C (G a i));
        ∃ (hH : ∀ (a : ι) (i : Fin c), (MvPolynomial.eval (y a)) (H a i) = 0) (hJ :
          ∀ (a : ι),

    Ideal.span (Set.range MvPowerSeries.X)

LinearStudy.smooth_point_constructs_original_linear_parameters.{u_1, u_2} {K : Type u_1} {σ : Type u_2} [Field K]
  [Fintype σ] [DecidableEq σ] (I P : Ideal (MvPolynomial σ K)) [P.IsPrime] (hIP : I ≤ P) (x : σ → K)
  (hP : P = RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval x).toRingHom)
  (hI : Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial σ K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I ≠ ⊥)
  [Algebra.FormallySmooth K
      (Localization.AtPrime P ⧸ Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial σ K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I)] :
  let J := Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial σ K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I;

    r = Module.finrank (Localization.AtPrime P ⧸ J) Ω[Localization.AtPrime P ⧸ J⁄K] ∧
      (∀ (i : Fin r), (a i).totalDegree ≤ 1 ∧ (MvPolynomial.eval x) (a i) = 0) ∧
        Ideal.span
            (Set.range fun i =>
              (Ideal.Quotient.mk J) ((algebraMap (MvPolynomial σ K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) (a i))) =
          IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal (Localization.AtPrime P ⧸ J)

  [Algebra.FormallySmooth K
      (Localization.AtPrime P ⧸ Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial σ K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I)] :

  (I J P Q : Ideal (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ)) [P.IsPrime] [Q.IsPrime]
  [IsArtinianRing (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ ⧸ J)] (q : MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ ⧸ J →ₐ[ℂ] ℂ)
  (x : Fin r ⊕ Fin c → ℂ) (hx : x = fun i => q ((Ideal.Quotient.mk J) (MvPolynomial.X i)))
  (F : Fin r ⊕ Fin c → MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (hP : P = RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval x).toRingHom)
  (hQ : Q = RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval fun i => (MvPolynomial.eval x) (F i)).toRingHom)
  (hrad : (Ideal.map (MvPolynomial.aeval F).toRingHom I).radical = I) (G H : Fin c → MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ)
  (hG : ∀ (i : Fin c), (MvPolynomial.eval x) (G i) = 0)
  (hJac : IsUnit (Matrix.det fun i j => (MvPolynomial.eval x) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) (G i))))
  (hsource :
    Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I =
      Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime P)) (G i)))
  (htarget :
    Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime Q)) I =
      Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ) (Localization.AtPrime Q)) (H i)))
  (τ : Fin r → MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ)
  (hJ : J = Ideal.map (MvPolynomial.aeval F).toRingHom I ⊔ Ideal.span (Set.range τ)) (hr : 0 < r) (hc : 0 < c)
  (hτ :
    Ideal.span
        (Set.range fun i => MvPowerSeries.constantCoeff ((LinearStudy.polynomialSmoothFormalMap x G hG hJac) (τ i))) =
      IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal (LinearStudy.ParameterRing r)) :
  have θ := Matrix.det fun i j => (MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) ((MvPolynomial.aeval F) (H i));
  have a :=
    (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) ℂ ⧸ J) (Localization.AtPrime (RingHom.ker q.toRingHom)))
      ((Ideal.Quotient.mk J) θ);
  Submodule.annihilator (nilradical (Localization.AtPrime (RingHom.ker q.toRingHom))) = Ideal.span {a} ∧ a ≠ 0

  [p.IsPrime] [Algebra.IsSmoothAt K p] (x : σ → K)
  (hp : Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk I) p = RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval x).toRingHom) :
  ∃ r c,
    ∃ (_ : 0 < c),
      ∃ e M,
        ∃ (hM : M.det ≠ 0),
          ∃ H,
            (∀ (i : Fin c),
                H i - MvPowerSeries.X (Sum.inr i) ∈ IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal (MvPowerSeries (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) ^ 2) ∧
              Ideal.map
                  (((LinearStudy.formalPolynomialAtPoint (M⁻¹.mulVec (x ∘ ⇑e))).comp
                        (LinearStudy.polynomialLinearChangeEquiv M hM).toRingEquiv.toRingHom).comp
                    (MvPolynomial.rename ⇑e.symm).toRingHom)
                  I =
                Ideal.span (Set.range H)

LinearStudy.smooth_point_constructs_original_polynomial_parameters.{u_1, u_2} {K : Type u_1} {σ : Type u_2} [Field K]
  [Fintype σ] [DecidableEq σ] (I P : Ideal (MvPolynomial σ K)) [P.IsPrime] (hIP : I ≤ P) (x : σ → K)
  (hP : P = RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval x).toRingHom)
  (hI : Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial σ K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I ≠ ⊥)
  [Algebra.FormallySmooth K
      (Localization.AtPrime P ⧸ Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial σ K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I)] :
  let J := Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial σ K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I;
  ∃ r a,
    r = Module.finrank (Localization.AtPrime P ⧸ J) Ω[Localization.AtPrime P ⧸ J⁄K] ∧
      Ideal.span
          (Set.range fun i => (Ideal.Quotient.mk J) ((algebraMap (MvPolynomial σ K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) (a i))) =
        IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal (Localization.AtPrime P ⧸ J)

LinearStudy.smooth_target_constructs_unramified_pullback_parameters.{u_1} {K : Type u_1} [Field K] {r c : ℕ}
  [Nonempty (Fin r)] [Nonempty (Fin c)] (I P : Ideal (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K)) [I.IsPrime] [P.IsPrime]
  (hI : I ≠ ⊥) (p : Ideal (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K ⧸ I)) [p.IsPrime] [Algebra.IsSmoothAt K p]
  (x y : Fin r ⊕ Fin c → K) (hP : P = RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval x).toRingHom)
  (hp : Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk I) p = RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval y).toRingHom)
  (hr : r = Module.finrank (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K ⧸ I) Ω[MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K ⧸ I⁄K])
  (F : Fin r ⊕ Fin c → MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K)
  (hQP : Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk I) p = Ideal.comap (MvPolynomial.aeval F).toRingHom P)
  (hF : Ideal.map (MvPolynomial.aeval F).toRingHom I ≤ I) (G : Fin c → MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K)
  (hG : ∀ (i : Fin c), (MvPolynomial.eval x) (G i) = 0)
  (hJ : IsUnit (Matrix.det fun i j => (MvPolynomial.eval x) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) (G i))))
  (hlocal :
    Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I =
      Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) (G i)))
  (hfinite :
    Algebra.EssFiniteType
      (Localization.AtPrime (Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk I) p) ⧸
        Ideal.map
          (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.AtPrime (Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk I) p))) I)
      (Localization.AtPrime P ⧸ Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I))
  (hunram :
    Algebra.FormallyUnramified
      (Localization.AtPrime (Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk I) p) ⧸
        Ideal.map
          (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.AtPrime (Ideal.comap (Ideal.Quotient.mk I) p))) I)
      (Localization.AtPrime P ⧸ Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I)) :
  ∃ a,
    have T := fun i => (LinearStudy.polynomialSmoothReducedMap x G hG hJ) ((MvPolynomial.aeval F) (a i));
    Ideal.span (Set.range T) = IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal (MvPowerSeries (Fin r) K) ∧
      IsUnit (Matrix.det fun i j => MvPowerSeries.constantCoeff ((MvPowerSeries.pderiv j) (T i)))

  [Fintype α] [Fintype β] [DecidableEq α] [DecidableEq β] (A : Matrix β (α ⊕ β) K)
  (hJ : IsUnit (A.submatrix id Sum.inr).det) :
  have B := A.submatrix id Sum.inl;
  have J := A.submatrix id Sum.inr;
  have M := Matrix.fromBlocks 1 0 (-(J⁻¹ * B)) 1;
  M.det = 1 ∧ A * M = Matrix.fromCols 0 J

  [Field K] [Fintype α] [Fintype β] [DecidableEq α] [DecidableEq β] (G : β → MvPolynomial (α ⊕ β) K) (x : α ⊕ β → K)

LinearStudy.formalThickening_relativeJacobian_socle {r c : ℕ} (H : Fin c → LinearStudy.AmbientRing r c) (hr : 0 < r)
  (hc : 0 < c) (hrad : (LinearStudy.equationIdeal H).radical = Ideal.span (Set.range MvPowerSeries.X)) :
  have q := LinearStudy.coordinateThickeningReductionFromRadical H hc hrad;
  (∀ (x : LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection H),
      LinearStudy.Annihilates (nilradical (LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection H)) x ↔
        ∃ b, x = b • LinearStudy.relativeJacobian H) ∧
    ∀ (τ : Fin r → LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection H),
      Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => q (τ i)) = IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal (LinearStudy.ParameterRing r) →
        have J := Ideal.span (Set.range τ);
        let Q := LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection H ⧸ J;
        have m := Ideal.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk J) (nilradical (LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection H));
        IsArtinianRing Q ∧
          m.IsMaximal ∧
            Submodule.annihilator m = Ideal.span {(Ideal.Quotient.mk J) (LinearStudy.relativeJacobian H)} ∧
              (Ideal.Quotient.mk J) (LinearStudy.relativeJacobian H) ≠ 0

LinearStudy.polynomial_universal_difference_matrix.{u_1, u_2} {R : Type u_1} {ι : Type u_2} [CommRing R] [Fintype ι]
  [DecidableEq ι] (P : ι → MvPolynomial ι R) (hzero : ∀ (i : ι), MvPolynomial.constantCoeff (P i) = 0) :
  ∃ D,
    ((D.mulVec fun i => MvPolynomial.X (Sum.inl i) - MvPolynomial.X (Sum.inr i)) = fun i =>
        (MvPolynomial.rename Sum.inl) (P i) - (MvPolynomial.rename Sum.inr) (P i)) ∧
      (∀ (i j : ι), LinearStudy.polynomialDoubleDiagonal (D i j) = (MvPolynomial.pderiv j) (P i)) ∧
        (D.map ⇑LinearStudy.polynomialDoubleRightZero).mulVec MvPowerSeries.X = fun i => ↑(P i)

LinearStudy.unramified_completed_parameters_coordinates.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {R : Type u_1} {S : Type u_2} {K : Type u_3}
  {r : ℕ} [CommRing R] [CommRing S] [Field K] [Algebra R S] [IsLocalRing R] [IsLocalRing S] [IsNoetherianRing S]
  [IsLocalHom (algebraMap R S)] [Algebra.EssFiniteType R S] [Algebra.FormallyUnramified R S] (t : Fin r → R)
  (ht : Ideal.span (Set.range t) = IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal R)
  (e : AdicCompletion (IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal S) S ≃+* MvPowerSeries (Fin r) K) :
  let C := AdicCompletion (IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal S) S;
  have T := fun i => e ((algebraMap S C) ((algebraMap R S) (t i)));
  Ideal.span (Set.range T) = IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal (MvPowerSeries (Fin r) K) ∧
    IsUnit (Matrix.det fun i j => MvPowerSeries.constantCoeff ((MvPowerSeries.pderiv j) (T i)))
'LinearStudy.unramified_completed_parameters_coordinates' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
LinearStudy.smooth_local_unramified_parameters_generate.{u_1, u_2} {K : Type u_1} [Field K] {r c : ℕ} [Nonempty (Fin c)]
  [Nonempty (Fin r)] (I P : Ideal (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K)) [P.IsPrime] (x : Fin r ⊕ Fin c → K)
  (hP : P = RingHom.ker (MvPolynomial.aeval x).toRingHom) (G : Fin c → MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K)
  (hG : ∀ (i : Fin c), (MvPolynomial.eval x) (G i) = 0)
  (hJ : IsUnit (Matrix.det fun i j => (MvPolynomial.eval x) ((MvPolynomial.pderiv (Sum.inr j)) (G i))))
  (hlocal :
    Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I =
      Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) (G i)))
  (R : Type u_2) [CommRing R] [IsLocalRing R]
  [Algebra R
      (Localization.AtPrime P ⧸ Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I)]
  [IsLocalHom
      (algebraMap R
        (Localization.AtPrime P ⧸ Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I))]
  [Algebra.EssFiniteType R
      (Localization.AtPrime P ⧸ Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I)]
  [Algebra.FormallyUnramified R
      (Localization.AtPrime P ⧸ Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I)]
  (t : Fin r → R) (ht : Ideal.span (Set.range t) = IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal R) :
  let S := Localization.AtPrime P ⧸ Ideal.map (algebraMap (MvPolynomial (Fin r ⊕ Fin c) K) (Localization.AtPrime P)) I;
  have φ := LinearStudy.polynomialSmoothLocalQuotientReduction I P x hP G hG hJ hlocal;
  have T := fun i => φ ((algebraMap R S) (t i));
  Ideal.span (Set.range T) = IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal (MvPowerSeries (Fin r) K) ∧
    IsUnit (Matrix.det fun i j => MvPowerSeries.constantCoeff ((MvPowerSeries.pderiv j) (T i)))
'LinearStudy.smooth_local_unramified_parameters_generate' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]

ExteriorAlgebra.prodEquivProd_apply.{u, u_1} (R : Type u) [CommRing R] (M : Type u_1) [AddCommGroup M] [Module R M]
  (x y : ExteriorAlgebra R M) :
  (ExteriorAlgebra.prodEquivProd R M) (x, y) =
    (ExteriorAlgebra.map (LinearMap.inl R M R)) x +
      (ExteriorAlgebra.map (LinearMap.inl R M R)) y * (ExteriorAlgebra.ι R) (0, 1)

def LinearStudy.LinearityTheoremGoal : Prop :=
∀ (n : ℕ) (f : LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n) (V : LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n),
  1 < f.degree → Function.Surjective f.onPoints → f.onPoints ⁻¹' V.zeroSet = V.zeroSet → ∃ W, ↑W = V.zeroSet
structure LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism (n : ℕ) : Type
number of parameters: 1
fields:
  LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism.degree : ℕ
  LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism.forms : Fin (n + 1) → LinearStudy.CoordinateRing n
  LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism.homogeneous : ∀ (i : Fin (n + 1)),
      MvPolynomial.IsHomogeneous (self.forms i) self.degree
  LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism.noBasePoint : ∀ (v : LinearStudy.CoordinateVector n),
      v ≠ 0 → (fun i => (MvPolynomial.eval v) (self.forms i)) ≠ 0
constructor:
  LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism.mk {n : ℕ} (degree : ℕ) (forms : Fin (n + 1) → LinearStudy.CoordinateRing n)
    (homogeneous : ∀ (i : Fin (n + 1)), MvPolynomial.IsHomogeneous (forms i) degree)
    (noBasePoint : ∀ (v : LinearStudy.CoordinateVector n), v ≠ 0 → (fun i => (MvPolynomial.eval v) (forms i)) ≠ 0) :
    LinearStudy.HomogeneousEndomorphism n
structure LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations (n : ℕ) : Type
number of parameters: 1
fields:
  LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations.ideal : HomogeneousIdeal (MvPolynomial.homogeneousSubmodule (Fin (n + 1)) ℂ)
  LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations.prime : self.ideal.toIdeal.IsPrime
  LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations.nonempty : ∃ v, v ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ P ∈ self.ideal.toIdeal, (MvPolynomial.eval v) P = 0
constructor:
  LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations.mk {n : ℕ}
    (ideal : HomogeneousIdeal (MvPolynomial.homogeneousSubmodule (Fin (n + 1)) ℂ)) (prime : ideal.toIdeal.IsPrime)
    (nonempty : ∃ v, v ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ P ∈ ideal.toIdeal, (MvPolynomial.eval v) P = 0) :
    LinearStudy.IntegralProjectiveEquations n
def LinearStudy.Lemma31Goal : ℕ → ℕ → Prop :=
fun r c =>
  ∀ (H : Fin c → LinearStudy.AmbientRing r c),
    0 < r →
      0 < c →
        RingTheory.Sequence.IsRegular (LinearStudy.AmbientRing r c) (List.ofFn H) →
          Module.Finite (LinearStudy.ParameterRing r) (LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection H) →
            Module.Flat (LinearStudy.ParameterRing r) (LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection H) →
              ∀ (q : LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection H →ₐ[LinearStudy.ParameterRing r] LinearStudy.ParameterRing r),
                RingHom.ker q.toRingHom = nilradical (LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection H) →
                  (∀ (x : LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection H),
                      LinearStudy.Annihilates (nilradical (LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection H)) x ↔
                        ∃ b, x = b • LinearStudy.relativeJacobian H) ∧
                    ∀ (τ : Fin r → LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection H),
                      Ideal.span (Set.range fun i => q (τ i)) = IsLocalRing.maximalIdeal (LinearStudy.ParameterRing r) →
                        let J := Ideal.span (Set.range τ);
                        let Q := LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection H ⧸ J;
                        have m := Ideal.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk J) (nilradical (LinearStudy.CompleteIntersection H));
                        IsArtinianRing Q ∧
                          m.IsMaximal ∧
                            Submodule.annihilator m =
                                Ideal.span {(Ideal.Quotient.mk J) (LinearStudy.relativeJacobian H)} ∧
                              (Ideal.Quotient.mk J) (LinearStudy.relativeJacobian H) ≠ 0
theorem LinearStudy.lemma31_complete : ∀ (r c : ℕ), LinearStudy.Lemma31Goal r c :=
⋯
